# Serbian, B2

1404 meanings: 1091 ok, 291 collision, 17 quarantined, 5 duplicate synonyms in Serbian.
Translated 1404 (v1 kept 0); A and B agreed on 986/1404 (70%); accepted without adjudication 910/1404 (65%); adjudicated 487 (a 307, b 119, other 57, unsure 4); level overrides 1.

Conventions: nouns in the nominative singular, adjectives masculine singular, Russian and Serbian verbs as the imperfective infinitive with the perfective partner in the note (`pf:`), Serbian Ekavian in Cyrillic, Russian with ё, English in British spelling. Notes: `g` gender (only where the ending doesn't show it; `pl` = plural-only), `pl` irregular plural, `pf` perfective partner, `past` English irregular forms. Hints are the sense notes shown with a translation where several meanings share a term. Statuses: `ok` (offered to learners of the language), `collision` (another meaning has the same word in this language: shown to its native speakers, not offered to its learners), `quarantined` (failed verification: never shown), counted apart when it is a `duplicate synonym` (another meaning has the same term in this language and no hint tells them apart: the other one keeps it).

## Adjudications

| key | English (sense) | a | b | verdict → term | status | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `spell_v` | spell (write or name letters in order) | срицати | срицати | a → изговарати слово по слово | ok | срицати is the dictionary verb for spelling out letters in order; B's aspect partner срецати is wrong. · judge: pass: Изговарати слово по слово is an imperfective fixed expression for spelling aloud, acceptable since no common single verb exists. |
| `major_n` | major (main subject of university study) | смер | студијска група | a → смер | collision | Смер is the established word for a university course of study in Serbia; студијска група is a clumsy phrase. |
| `fine_v` | fine (impose a monetary penalty on someone) | кажњавати | казнити новчано | a → кажњавати | ok | Imperfective single verb; B is a perfective phrase flagged hard, and speeding fines are usually kažnjavati novčano. |
| `cool_v` | cool (to become less hot) | хладити | хладити се | b → хладити се | ok | Soup cooling by itself is intransitive, so reflexive хладити се fits; plain хладити is transitive, meaning to cool something. |
| `sound_adj` | sound (reliable and well-founded) | поуздан | чврст | a → поуздан | collision | Sound advice is reliable, trustworthy counsel; поуздан expresses that, while чврст means physically firm. |
| `trade_v` | trade (to exchange goods or services for money) | трговати | трговати | a → трговати | ok | Imperfective verb for exchanging goods; b's aspect partner продати means 'sell', a different meaning, so a's entry is cleaner. |
| `chief_n` | chief (leader or head of a group) | шеф | вођа | a → шеф | collision | The chief who makes the final decision is the head or boss; шеф is the very common word, while вођа means leader generally. |
| `store_v` | store (keep for future use) | чувати | складиштити | a → чувати | collision | Чувати is the imperfective everyday verb for keeping food for later; складиштити is flagged perfective and suits warehouses. |
| `double_v` | double (become twice as much) | удвостручивати се | удвостручити се | a → удвостручивати се | ok | Price doubling is a process; B удвостручити се is perfective, so imperfective удвостручивати се is the term. |
| `potential_adj` | potential (possible or likely to happen) | потенцијалан | потенцијални | a → потенцијалан | ok | Potential risk uses the dictionary form потенцијалан; the definite form потенцијални is not the lemma required. |
| `particular_adj` | particular (specific or special) | конкретан | посебан | b → посебан | collision | A particular reason for asking is a special reason, посебан разлог; конкретан means concrete or tangible and is narrower. |
| `sort_v` | sort (arrange things into groups) | сортирати | разврставати | a → сортирати | ok | Sorting laundry by color is сортирати, a concrete everyday verb; разврставати is more bookish classifying. |
| `short_term_adj` | short-term (lasting for a short time) | краткорочан | краткорочни | a → краткорочан | ok | Dictionary adjective form is the indefinite masculine краткорочан; краткорочни is the definite form, which the rules do not allow as lemma. |
| `brother_in_law_n` | brother-in-law (sister's husband) | шогор | зет | b → зет | collision | Зет is the common word in Serbia for sister's husband, the most frequent brother-in-law; шогор is regional and colloquial. · judge: fail: Шурак means only the wife's brother, and the gloss wrongly adds the husband's brother, who is девер; the term cannot cover the full sense. · term override: шурак (quarantined) is only the wife's brother; зет, the sister's husband, is one of brother-in-law's meanings and matches Russian зять |
| `will_n` | will (legal document about inheritance) | тестамент | опорука | a → тестамент | ok | Тестамент is the common Serbian word for a will in Serbia; опорука is rarer and more Croatian. |
| `last_minute_adj` | last-minute (done at the very end of time) | последњи тренутак | последњег тренутка | b → последњег тренутка | ok | As a modifier ('одлука последњег тренутка'), the genitive phrase works attributively; a's nominative form cannot qualify a noun. |
| `stock_n` | stock (liquid base for soup) | супа | супа од костију | other → бујон | ok | Chicken stock added to soup is бујон; супа is the finished dish itself, and супа од костију is a descriptive phrase. · judge: pass: бујон is the established Serbian noun for broth or stock made from meat and bones, fitting the soup example. · term override: бујон for stock needs the hint: stock is mostly goods or shares |
| `lack_v` | lack (to not have enough of something) | недостајати | недостатак | a → недостајати | collision | We lack skills is недостају нам вештине, the verb недостајати; b's недостатак is a noun and breaks the part of speech. |
| `daughter_in_law_n` | daughter-in-law (son's wife) | снаха | снаја | a → снаха | ok | Снаха is the standard word for a son's wife; the other answer wrongly narrows the gloss to an only son. · term override: снаха is also a brother's wife: the hint says the son's |
| `overall_adv` | overall (considering all aspects together) | уопште | укупно | other → у целини | ok | Sentence adverb 'overall' means on the whole; уопште means in general or at all, and укупно means in total amount. · judge: pass: у целини is a standard adverbial phrase for considering everything together; acceptable as a short fixed expression for overall. |
| `challenge_v` | challenge (test someone's abilities or skills) | изазивати | изазивати | a → изазивати | collision | Imperfective изазивати with partner изазвати is correct aspect; it covers testing someone's abilities, as the exam challenged him. |
| `primary_adj` | primary (most important or main) | главни | примарни | a → главни | collision | The example 'primary reason' wants the plain everyday word for main; примарни is more technical, used for education or medicine. |
| `environment_n` | environment (the natural world and surroundings) | животна средина | околина | a → животна средина | ok | Example 'protect the environment' needs the ecological sense, which животна средина names; околина means mere surroundings. |
| `executive_n` | executive (a senior manager in a company) | директор | извршни директор | a → директор | collision | A senior manager signing a deal is a директор; извршни директор is a specific title meaning CEO, narrower than the sense. |
| `investment_n` | investment (money put into business for profit) | инвестиција | улагање | a → инвестиција | ok | Her investment in stocks is money placed for profit as a thing; инвестиција is the common noun, улагање stresses the action. |
| `sister_in_law_n` | sister-in-law (brother's wife) | снаха | свестрима | unsure → снаха | collision | Serbian splits this by relation: заова, свастика, јетрва, снаха; снаха often means daughter-in-law and свестрима is not established, so no single word fits. · term override: снаха, the brother's wife, is one of sister-in-law's meanings (the husband's sister is заова, the wife's свастика); the hint says which |
| `budget_adj` | budget (low cost and inexpensive) | јефтин | јефтин | a → јефтин | quarantined | Both identical; јефтин is the natural Serbian modifier for a budget hotel, since буџетски is not an established usage. |
| `estate_n` | estate (large area of land with a large house) | имање | посед | a → имање | ok | имање is the common neutral word for a landed property with a house; посед stresses ownership and is bookish. |
| `profile_n` | profile (set of information about a person online) | профил | профил | b → профил | ok | Same term; b's gloss, online personal data, captures the social media sense that a's vague 'information about a person' misses. |
| `property_n_characteristic_feature` | property (a characteristic feature of something) | својство | особина | a → својство | ok | Својство is the attribute or property of a thing or relation, as in the example; особина is more a personal trait. |
| `prime_adj` | prime (most important or valuable) | главни | примаран | other → прворазредан | ok | A prime location means top-quality, first-class; главни would mean main, and примаран is not used for desirable property. Прворазредан is the established word. · judge: pass: прворазредан is an established adjective for top-quality things, fitting a prime location. |
| `spread_v` | spread (to distribute over an area) | мазати | размазивати | a → мазати | ok | Spreading jam on toast is мазати, imperfective and the everyday verb; размазивати implies smearing around. |
| `living_n` | living (money needed for daily expenses) | живот | издржавање | a → средства за живот | ok | Make a living is зарађивати за живот; издржавање means support or alimony paid for dependents. · judge: pass: Средства за живот is an established expression for means of livelihood, fitting earning enough to live on. |
| `long_distance_adj` | long-distance (covering a great distance) | далекосежни | дугачки | other → — | quarantined | далекосежни means far-reaching in effect and дугачки means physically long; међуградски is the established adjective for long-distance buses and calls. · judge: fail: Међуградски means intercity, which is a different notion because an intercity route can be short. Long-distance, covering a great distance, would need дугопругашки or a similar word. |
| `cast_v` | cast (to choose actors for a role) | бирати | улазити | other → додељивати улогу | ok | Casting her as the main character means assigning a role; улазити is wrong and бирати alone loses the acting sense. · judge: pass: додељивати улогу is a natural fixed phrase for assigning a role, since Serbian has no single common imperfective verb for casting. |
| `criminal_adj` | criminal (related to crime or law breaking) | криминални | кривични | a → криминалан | ok | Криминални is the general adjective for crime-related things; кривични is mainly legal, as in penal law or procedure. · judge: pass: Криминалан is a common adjective, in dictionary masculine form, relating to crime and fitting a criminal record. |
| `real_estate_n` | real estate (land and buildings owned by someone) | некретнине | некретнина | b → некретнина | collision | Dictionary form is the singular feminine некретнина, which Wiktionary lists; the plural form is only its common usage. |
| `surprise_v` | surprise (to cause someone to feel surprise) | изненадити | изненадити | other → изненађивати | ok | Both answers are perfective изненадити; the imperfective изненађивати is the required term for the news surprising everyone. · judge: pass: изненађивати is the common imperfective verb for surprising someone, Ekavian and correct. |
| `technical_adj` | technical (relating to a specific subject or skill) | технички | технички | a → технички | ok | Both answers are identical; технички is the standard adjective for specialist, jargon-heavy instructions. |
| `treat_v` | treat (give special care or attention to) | односити се | поступати | a → односити се | ok | Односити се према некоме fits behaving with respect toward everyone; поступати concerns acting or proceeding, less personal. |
| `evil_adj` | evil (very bad or morally wrong) | зао | злочи | a → зао | collision | зао is the ordinary adjective for morally bad; B злочи is not an established word. |
| `reference_n` | reference (mentioning or relating to something) | помињање | помињање | a → помињање | ok | Example says no reference was made, i.e. no mention; помињање is an established noun for mentioning, and A and B give the same word. |
| `core_n` | core (central or most important part) | срж | суштина | a → срж | ok | Core of a relationship means its central part; срж is the established figurative word, суштина leans toward essence or gist. |
| `mention_v` | mention (to speak about something briefly) | спомињати | помињати | a → спомињати | ok | Imperfective of споменути and the common form for briefly referring to a problem; помињати is rarer, more archaic and often negative. |
| `stress_v` | stress (emphasize or give importance to) | наглашавати | нагласити | a → наглашавати | ok | Imperfective наглашавати means emphasize an importance; b нагласити is perfective and not allowed as the term. |
| `initial_adj` | initial (happening at the beginning) | почетни | првобитан | a → почетни | ok | Почетни results are those at the beginning; првобитан means original or former, a different sense. |
| `mail_v` | mail (to send a letter or package) | слати | послати | a → слати | collision | Imperfective with partner послати; answer b is perfective, and слати поштом fits mailing a letter. |
| `shape_v` | shape (influence how something develops) | обликовати | обликовати | b → обликовати | ok | Обликовати works for shaping character or development and is biaspectual, so the perfective flag is wrong; b's gloss is fuller. |
| `transfer_n` | transfer (act of moving to a different place or job) | премештај | прелазак | a → премештај | ok | Премештај is the official word for a job or office transfer; прелазак means a crossing or a switch. |
| `beat_v_win_against` | beat (win against someone in a game) | победити | победити | other → побеђивати | collision | Both answers give perfective победити; the imperfective infinitive required for defeating a team is побеђивати. · judge: pass: побеђивати is the standard imperfective verb for defeating an opponent, fitting beating the other team. |
| `promise_v` | promise (say you will definitely do something) | обећавати | обећати | a → обећавати | ok | The imperfective infinitive is required; answer b gave perfective обећати, while обећавати fits 'I promise to call'. |
| `purchase_v` | purchase (to buy something) | купити | куповати | b → куповати | collision | Imperfective infinitive required; a is perfective купити. Covers purchasing supplies. |
| `profit_n` | profit (money gained from business after costs) | профит | добит | b → добит | ok | Добит is the standard accounting word for profit after costs, feminine in Serbian; профит is a more colloquial loanword. |
| `loan_n` | loan (money borrowed from a bank) | кредит | зајам | a → кредит | collision | Money from a bank to buy a car is кредит in everyday Serbian; зајам is more general or private. |
| `appeal_v` | appeal (to make a serious or urgent request) | апеловати | апелирати | a → апеловати | ok | A charity appealing for donations is апеловати; B's aspect partner апликовати is a wrong verb meaning to apply. |
| `route_n` | route (way or path to a destination) | пут | путања | a → пут | collision | The shortest way home is најкраћи пут (zipf 5.82); путања means a trajectory of a moving object. |
| `squad_n` | squad (small group of people working together) | одред | екипа | b → екипа | ok | A rescue squad is a working crew, спасилачка екипа; одред suggests a military or scout detachment. |
| `concern_v` | concern (affect or worry someone) | забринути | дотичати | other → забрињавати | ok | News worrying many people needs the imperfective забрињавати; a's забринути is perfective and b's дотичати is a rare bookish form. · judge: pass: забрињавати is the established imperfective verb for causing worry, fits the news example. |
| `strike_v` | strike (hit something hard) | ударити | ударати | b → ударати | collision | Imperfective ударати with partner ударити; a is perfective. A ball hitting a window fits this sense. |
| `claim_n_request_compensation` | claim (request for compensation) | одштетни захтев | тужба | a → одштетни захтев | ok | An insurance claim is a demand for compensation; одштетни захтев is the exact term, while тужба is a lawsuit. |
| `climate_change_n` | climate change (long-term warming of Earth) | климатске промене | промена климе | a → климатске промене | ok | Климатске промене is the fixed plural-only term used in Serbian media and science; the genitive phrase промена климе is less usual. |
| `equal_v` | equal (be the same as) | бити једнак | једнакити се | a → бити једнак | ok | Sum example 'equals four' is expressed by бити једнак; једнакити се is not an established verb in this sense. |
| `split_v` | split (to divide into parts) | поделити | делити | b → делити | collision | Imperfective делити fits cutting a cake in two; поделити in answer a is perfective. |
| `aircraft_n` | aircraft (plane or other flying vehicle) | ваздухоплов | авион | other → летелица | ok | Ваздухоплов is bookish and rare; авион is only an airplane; летелица covers planes and other flying vehicles in Serbia. · judge: pass: летелица is the established Serbian noun for an aircraft or flying vehicle, fitting the landing example. |
| `corporate_adj` | corporate (relating to a large company) | корпоративни | корпоративан | b → корпоративан | ok | Dictionary lists the indefinite masculine form корпоративан as headword; а's definite form корпоративни is not the citation form. |
| `friendly_adj` | friendly (kind and pleasant to others) | љубазан | пријатељски | a → љубазан | collision | Neighbours who are kind and pleasant are љубазни; пријатељски describes relations or gestures between friends, not a person's manner. |
| `smile_v` | smile (show happiness with your face) | смешити се | осмехивати се | a → смешити се | ok | смешити се is the Ekavian imperfective for smiling at a baby; насмешити се is its perfective partner. |
| `blame_n` | blame (responsibility for a bad thing) | кривица | одговорност | a → кривица | collision | Прихватити кривицу is the idiom for accepting blame; одговорност means responsibility and lacks the sense of fault. |
| `display_v` | display (to show something publicly) | приказивати | излагати | b → излагати | ok | A museum exhibits works, and излагати is the standard verb for showing objects publicly; приказивати suits films or data. |
| `struggle_v` | struggle (to try very hard to do something difficult) | борити се | борити се | other → мучити се | ok | Struggled to lift a heavy box is physical difficulty, not fighting; мучити се да fits, while борити се means combat or fight against. · judge: pass: мучити се is the common imperfective reflexive verb for struggling with effort, fits lifting the heavy box. |
| `assembly_n` | assembly (a group of people gathered together) | збор | скупштина | a → збор | ok | Збор names a gathered group of people such as a school assembly; скупштина is a formal parliament or legislative body. |
| `solo_adv` | solo (without other people or instruments) | соло | самостално | a → соло | ok | Соло is the established word for singing alone; B's самостално means independently and excludes the performance meaning. |
| `upset_v` | upset (make someone unhappy or worried) | узнемирити | узнемиравати | b → узнемиравати | collision | Imperfective with the right partner and covers worrying someone like a mother; a's узнемирити is perfective. |
| `related_adj` | related (connected by family or blood) | сродан | родбински | a → сродан | ok | Сродан is the adjective for being related by blood; родбински means pertaining to kin, not a predicate for a person. |
| `branch_v` | branch (to divide into parts) | разгранати се | разгранавати се | b → разгранавати се | ok | A is the perfective form; B is the imperfective with the perfective as partner, fitting a road that forks. |
| `plot_n` | plot (the main story of a book or film) | радња | заплет | a → радња | collision | Радња филма is the everyday collocation for a movie plot; заплет is narrower, the complication or knot of the story. |
| `mess_v` | mess (to make something dirty or untidy) | прљати | загадити | a → — | quarantined | Imperfective and established; covers making a room dirty, though it only partly captures untidiness, while загадити is perfective and means pollute. · judge: fail: Нередити is not an established standard verb; Serbian says правити неред or мешати, so this looks coined and is not common. |
| `foot_n_unit_length` | foot (unit of length equal to twelve inches) | стопа | стопа | a → фут | ok | Стопа is the established name for the imperial foot; both answers agree and the feminine gender is correct despite the Wiktionary tag. · judge: pass: Фут is the standard Serbian name for the unit of twelve inches, used in the room length example. |
| `index_n` | index (list of topics at the back of a book) | индекс | казало | a → индекс | ok | Индекс is the everyday word for a book's alphabetical index; казало is a rare purist word, and its back-translation drifted to contents. |
| `identify_v` | identify (recognize who or what someone is) | препознати | препознати | other → препознавати | collision | Both answers give perfective препознати; the required imperfective infinitive is препознавати, fitting recognizing the suspect. · judge: pass: препознавати is the common imperfective verb for recognizing who someone is, fits identifying a suspect. |
| `scheme_n` | scheme (secret plan to do something illegal) | шема | завјера | other → махинација | ok | Шема means diagram or pattern, and завјера is Ijekavian and means political conspiracy; махинација is an established word for an illicit fraud scheme. · judge: pass: махинација is a common noun for a secret dishonest scheme, fitting the fraud example. |
| `object_v` | object (express disagreement with) | противљење | противити се | b → противити се | collision | Противити се is a real imperfective verb matching 'I object to'; a's противљење is a noun, wrong part of speech. |
| `x_ray_n` | x-ray (medical image using radiation) | рендген | рентген | a → рендген | ok | Serbian standard spelling of the machine and image is рендген; рентген is the Croatian-style form. |
| `counter_n` | counter (long flat surface for transactions) | пулт | шино | a → пулт | ok | Checkout counter is a пулт in shops; шино is not an established Serbian word and its gloss is made up. |
| `creation_n` | creation (thing that has been made or invented) | творевина | дело | b → дело | ok | For a painting as his latest creation, дело is the natural everyday word; творевина is bookish and often means a mere product. |
| `somewhat_adv` | somewhat (to a moderate degree) | понешто | прилично | other → донекле | ok | Somewhat difficult means to a moderate extent; донекле fits, понешто leans to a little, and прилично means fairly or quite. · judge: pass: донекле is a common standard adverb meaning to some extent, fits somewhat difficult. |
| `mate_n` | mate (friend or companion) | друг | пријатељ | b → пријатељ | collision | Best mate from school is a close friend; пријатељ is the neutral common word, while друг carries comrade or classmate overtones. |
| `panel_n` | panel (group of experts) | панел | комисија | a → панел | ok | The example is a discussion panel of experts, where Serbian uses панел (as in панел дискусија); комисија implies a formal committee. |
| `relief_n` | relief (feeling of comfort) | олакашње | олакшање | b → олакшање | ok | Олакшање is the correct spelling of the noun for relief after an exam; a's олакашње is a misspelling. |
| `burn_v` | burn (to be damaged by fire or heat) | опећи | спалити | other → — | quarantined | Not burning your hand is injuring skin by heat, опекати; both answers are perfective (опећи, спалити), and спалити means burning something down. · judge: fail: опекати means to scorch or sear by contact and is flagged aspectually unreliable; the common verb for being burned is опећи се or горети. |
| `secondary_adj` | secondary (less important than the main thing) | споредни | споредан | b → споредан | ok | Dictionary form is indefinite masculine споредан; a's споредни is the definite form. |
| `expansion_n` | expansion (the act of becoming larger or more extensive) | ширење | експанзија | b → експанзија | ok | A company's major expansion is business growth, for which експанзија is the established noun; ширење suggests spreading. |
| `portion_n` | portion (a part or share of something) | део | део | b → део | collision | Both give део; sense is a part of something, as in a large part of the cake, which b's gloss reflects. |
