| `folk_n` | folk (people in general or a community) | људи | народ | a → људи | collision | Local folk being friendly means ordinary local people, which људи renders; народ means a nation or the common masses. |
| `interpretation_n` | interpretation (way of understanding something) | тумачење | интерпретација | a → тумачење | ok | Тумачење is the native word for a way of understanding something; интерпретација is a foreign loan more tied to art. |
| `nasty_adj` | nasty (very unpleasant or dirty) | одвратан | гадан | b → гадан | ok | Nasty weather is unpleasant, not disgusting; гадан covers unpleasant or dirty, while одвратан is stronger revulsion. |
| `slight_adj` | slight (small in degree or amount) | мали | мањи | other → незнатан | ok | Slight delay means minor in degree; незнатан is the established adjective for that, whereas мали means size and мањи is comparative. · judge: pass: незнатан is an established adjective for small in degree, masculine nominative, fits a slight delay. |
| `sue_v` | sue (take legal action against someone) | тужити | тужити | a → тужити | ok | Тужити is an imperfective verb meaning to sue; the perfective flag is mistaken. Both answers are identical. |
| `twist_n` | twist (a sudden change in a story or event) | обрт | завртетак | a → обрт | ok | Обрт is the established word for a plot twist; завртетак means a screw turn, not a story change. |
| `twist_v` | twist (turn something around its axis) | увртати | окретати | b → окретати | ok | Окретати fits turning a cap about its axis; увртати means screwing in, wrong direction for removing a cap. |
| `creator_n` | creator (person who makes something new) | творац | стваралац | a → творац | ok | Творац is the most frequent word for the originator of an app or work; стваралац is a rarer synonym. |
| `identical_adj` | identical (exactly the same in every way) | истоветан | идентичан | b → идентичан | ok | Идентичан is the common neutral adjective for 'identical'; истоветан is bookish and much rarer. |
| `hood_n` | hood (cover for the engine of a car) | поклопац | капа | other → хауба | ok | Хауба is the usual Serbian word for a car's engine hood; поклопац is a generic lid and капа means cap or hat. · judge: pass: хауба is the usual Serbian word for a car's engine hood, correct noun and sense. |
| `nail_n` | nail (hard part on fingers or toes) | нокат | нокт | a → нокат | ok | Nominative singular of the standard word for fingernail; нокт is an unusual back-formation from the plural нокти. |
| `repeatedly_adv` | repeatedly (many times in a row) | више пута | поновљено | a → више пута | ok | Calling someone many times is expressed with the fixed phrase више пута; поновљено is a participle, not a normal adverb. |
| `skip_v` | skip (go past or avoid doing something) | прескакати | заобићи | a → прескакати | ok | Imperfective прескакати matches skipping breakfast; заобићи is perfective and means bypass, not omit a meal. |
| `snap_v` | snap (take a quick photograph) | усликати | снимити | other → — | quarantined | Both are perfective; сликати is the common imperfective for taking a photo, as in Можеш ли да нас сликаш. |
| `attract_v` | attract (cause interest or attention) | привлачити | привући пажњу | a → привлачити | ok | Imperfective single verb with partner привући; fits a shop window drawing people, while b's perfective phrase is wrong in aspect. |
| `dating_n` | dating (going on romantic dates with someone) | изласци | заљубљивање | unsure → — | quarantined | Изласци only means outings, заљубљивање means falling in love; no established noun clearly means dating, the usual loan being дејтинг. |
| `demonstrate_v` | demonstrate (show clearly how something works) | демонстрирати | показати како функционише | a → демонстрирати | ok | Single established verb, biaspectual, for showing software at work; b's multi-word phrase is perfective and awkward. |
| `flip_v` | flip (turn something over quickly) | превртати | окренути | other → окретати | collision | Flipping a pancake needs imperfective окретати; b's окренути is perfective and a's превртати suggests overturning or rummaging. · judge: pass: окретати is the common imperfective verb for turning something over, fits flipping a pancake. |
| `generous_adj` | generous (willing to give more than usual) | дарежљив | великодушан | a → дарежљив | ok | Generous with her time is willingness to give; дарежљив is the everyday word, великодушан stresses magnanimity. |
| `implement_v` | implement (put a plan into effect) | спроводити | спровести | a → спроводити | collision | Putting new rules into effect needs the imperfective infinitive спроводити; спровести is perfective and is the aspect partner. |
| `patch_n` | patch (small piece used to mend something) | закрпа | заплатка | a → закрпа | ok | Standard Serbian for a mending patch sewn on jeans is закрпа; заплатка is a Russian-style form. |
| `resist_v` | resist (try to prevent something by action) | опирати се | отпорати се | other → одупирати се | ok | Отпорати се is perfective and means to unstitch; одупирати се is the common imperfective for resisting change, more usual than опирати се. · judge: pass: Одупирати се is an imperfective reflexive verb meaning to resist, and одупирати се променама fits resisting change. |
| `privilege_n` | privilege (special right or advantage) | привилегија | повластица | a → привилегија | ok | Привилегија covers 'a privilege to meet you' as an honour; повластица means a concession or discount, narrower and formal. |
| `wrap_v` | wrap (cover or enclose something in paper or cloth) | умотавати | замотавати | a → умотавати | ok | Imperfective for wrapping a gift in paper; замотавати suggests bundling or winding something up, such as bandages. |
| `boot_n` | boot (storage space in a car) | главни простор | пратећи простор | other → пртљажник | ok | Both answers are invented phrases; пртљажник is the established word for a car's luggage compartment in Serbia. · judge: pass: пртљажник is the standard Serbian noun for a car boot or trunk. |
| `coastal_adj` | coastal (relating to the coast of a landmass) | обални | обалски | other → приморски | ok | Обални is mainly for shorelines of rivers and lakes, and обалски is nonstandard; приморски град is the usual phrase for a coastal town. · judge: pass: приморски is the standard adjective for coastal, masculine nominative, fits a coastal town. |
| `confusion_n` | confusion (state of being bewildered or unclear) | забуна | збуњеност | a → забуна | ok | 'There was much confusion' describes a general disorder, which забуна expresses; збуњеност is one person's bewilderment. |
| `distant_adj` | distant (far away in space or time) | удаљен | даљински | a → удаљен | collision | A distant village is far away; удаљен is the ordinary adjective, while даљински means remote-controlled or long-distance. |
| `emotion_n` | emotion (strong feeling like love or anger) | емоција | осећање | a → емоција | ok | She showed no emotion means displayed feeling; емоција names strong feelings like love or anger, while осећање is broader sensation. |
| `insight_n` | insight (deep understanding of a situation) | увид | увиђај | a → увид | ok | Увид is the usual noun in 'увид у проблем' (insight into the problem); увиђај means on-site investigation by police. |
| `qualify_v` | qualify (to meet the requirements for something) | испуњавати услове | испунити услове | a → испуњавати услове | ok | Imperfective fixed expression for meeting job requirements; b испунити услове is the perfective form and is flagged. |
| `nightmare_n` | nightmare (very frightening dream) | ноћна мора | кошмар | a → ноћна мора | ok | Ноћна мора is the native, most common term for a frightening dream; кошмар is a loan, more often figurative. |
| `widespread_adj` | widespread (found or distributed over a large area) | раширен | раширен | b → раширен | ok | Both give раширен; b's gloss 'присутан' captures a belief or support being present broadly, not only area size. |
| `accessible_adj` | accessible (easy to reach or enter) | доступан | приступачан | b → приступачан | collision | A shop reached easily by bus is physical access, which приступачан expresses; доступан leans toward availability. |
| `acknowledge_v` | acknowledge (admit something is true or exists) | признавати | признати | a → признавати | collision | Imperfective required; answer b is perfective признати, while признавати fits admitting a mistake. |
| `cherry_n` | cherry (small red or black fruit with a stone) | трешња | вишња | a → трешња | ok | A sweet cherry is трешња; вишња is the sour cherry, a different fruit. |
| `collapse_n` | collapse (act of falling down or giving way) | урушавање | колапс | a → урушавање | ok | Урушавање is the native noun for a structure falling in, as with a bridge; колапс leans toward systems, health or economies. |
| `examine_v` | examine (look at something carefully) | прегледати | испитати | a → прегледати | ok | Doctor examining a patient is прегледати, which dictionaries list as biaspectual; испитати is perfective and suggests questioning or testing. |
| `film_maker_n` | film-maker (person who makes films) | филмски стваралац | редитељ | a → филмски стваралац | ok | Covers anyone who makes films; редитељ means only the director, narrower than the sense given, though A is a fixed phrase. |
| `grain_n` | grain (small hard seeds of cereal plants) | жито | житарица | a → жито | ok | жито is the common collective word for cereal grain; житарица denotes the cereal plant family rather than the seeds themselves. |
| `identification_n` | identification (proof of who you are) | лична карта | документ | a → лична карта | collision | 'Show your identification' means an ID card; документ alone is too vague and not specifically proof of identity. |
| `proceed_v` | proceed (continue doing something) | наставити | наставити | other → настављати | collision | Both answers give perfective наставити; the imperfective настављати is required and fits continuing to the next page. · judge: pass: настављати is the common imperfective verb for continuing or going on, fitting proceeding to the next page. |
| `lap_n` | lap (upper legs when sitting) | крило | колено | a → крило | collision | Cat sleeping on her lap is на крилу; колено is the knee, a different body part, so b names the wrong thing. |
| `spray_v` | spray (scatter liquid in drops) | прскати | прскати | a → прскати | collision | Прскати is imperfective, used for watering plants; its partner is прснути, so the perfective flag is spurious. |
| `significance_n` | significance (importance or meaning) | значај | значај | a → значај | collision | Great significance of an event is importance, which значај expresses; both answers give the same noun, and the cross-check mismatch concerns значење. |
| `spray_n` | spray (liquid in small drops) | спреј | прскалица | a → спреј | ok | Hair spray is спреј in Serbian; прскалица is the device that sprays, not the product. |
| `artistic_adj` | artistic (having skill or interest in art) | уметнички | уметнички | b → уметнички | ok | Same term in both; b's gloss states the sense of a person with talent for art, as in the example, not merely art-related. |
| `barrel_n` | barrel (large cylindrical container for liquids) | буре | бачва | a → буре | ok | Буре is the common neutral word for a wine barrel; бачва is regional, mostly Vojvodina, and for large vats. |
| `beloved_adj` | beloved (very much loved by people) | вољен | омиљен | a → вољен | ok | Вољен means loved, matching 'very much loved by people'; омиљен means favourite, a different shade. |
| `bless_v` | bless (to make holy or wish good luck) | благословљати | благословити | a → благословљати | ok | Imperfective form with partner благословити; answer b is perfective and breaks the aspect rule. |
| `colonial_adj` | colonial (related to a colony) | колонијални | колонијалан | b → колонијалан | ok | Rule requires the indefinite masculine dictionary form колонијалан; колонијални is the definite form of the same word. |
| `contrary_adj` | contrary (opposite in nature or direction) | супротан | спротиван | a → супротан | ok | Супротан is the standard adjective used with 'to' (супротан нашим надањима); b's спротиван is a nonstandard variant, and a's gloss is merely imprecise. |
| `lease_n` | lease (contract to rent property) | закуп | уговор о закупу | a → закуп | ok | закуп is the single established noun for a lease; B is a longer phrase naming the contract. |
| `loyalty_n` | loyalty (strong support or faithfulness to someone) | оданост | верност | a → оданост | ok | Loyalty to the team is devoted allegiance, which оданост expresses; верност leans toward fidelity in marriage or promises. |
| `well_balanced_adj` | well-balanced (showing good judgment and stability) | уравнотежен | добро уравнотежен | a → уравнотежен | ok | A single adjective covering a stable, sensible person; добро уравнотежен adds a redundant adverb and is a phrase. |
| `bubble_n` | bubble (small sphere of gas in liquid or soap film) | мехур | мехурић | b → мехурић | ok | The example is a small gum bubble; мехурић is the diminutive for small bubbles, while мехур also means bladder or blister. |
| `developing_adj` | developing (becoming more advanced or mature) | развијен | у развоју | b → у развоју | ok | Земље у развоју is the fixed phrase for developing countries; развијен means already developed, the opposite meaning. |
| `exhibit_v` | exhibit (to show or display publicly) | излагати | излагати | b → излагати | collision | A museum showing art is излагати; both give the same verb, B's gloss 'приказивати јавно' states display, not exposure. |
| `icon_n` | icon (symbol representing a computer file) | иконица | икона | b → икона | ok | Икона is the usual interface term for a screen icon in Serbian software; иконица is a rare diminutive. |
| `preliminary_adj` | preliminary (done before the main event) | прелиминаран | припремни | a → прелиминаран | ok | Прелиминаран means coming before the main stage, as in a preliminary meeting; припремни means preparatory, a narrower sense. |
| `legislature_n` | legislature (group that makes laws for a country) | законодавно тело | скупштина | a → законодавно тело | ok | Legislature as a general law-making body fits законодавно тело; скупштина is any assembly and names one institution. |
| `retain_v` | retain (keep or hold onto) | задржати | задржати | other → задржавати | ok | Both gave perfective задржати; the imperfective задржавати is required and fits a company keeping its staff. · judge: pass: задржавати is the common imperfective verb for keeping or holding on to something, such as staff. |
| `rocky_adj` | rocky (full of rocks or uneven) | стеновит | каменист | b → каменист | ok | A path full of stones is каменит or каменист; стеновит describes cliffs and bare rock faces. |
| `sometime_adv` | sometime (at an unspecified time in the future or past) | некад | некада | b → некада | ok | Sometime next week is an unspecified time; некада is the full standard form, while некад is its clipped colloquial variant. |
| `yield_v` | yield (give way to pressure) | попустити | попустити | other → попуштати | ok | Both answers are perfective попустити; imperfective попуштати fits a branch giving way under weight or pressure. · judge: pass: попуштати is the common imperfective verb for giving way under pressure or load, fits the branch example. |
| `barrier_n` | barrier (obstruction preventing movement) | препрека | преграда | a → препрека | ok | Препрека denotes something obstructing movement, as the fence example requires; преграда is more a partition dividing space. |
| `burst_v` | burst (to break open suddenly) | пући | пуцати | b → пуцати | collision | Imperfective infinitive required for a balloon bursting; пући is perfective and listed as the partner by the flag. |
| `cattle_n` | cattle (farm animals kept for milk or meat) | говедо | стока | a → говедо | ok | Cattle strictly means bovines, which говедо names; стока is wider livestock including sheep and pigs. |
| `eliminate_v` | eliminate (to remove or get rid of something) | елиминисати | уклонити | a → елиминисати | ok | Елиминисати is biaspectual and common for removing all errors; уклонити is perfective and only means move away. |
| `medication_n` | medication (medicine taken for illness) | лек | лекови | a → лек | collision | Лек is the normal singular for medication taken with food; the plural-only form is unneeded for this uncountable-like use. |
| `strain_n` | strain (physical injury to muscle) | истегнуће | истезање | a → истегнуће | ok | Истегнуће is the established medical noun for a pulled muscle; истезање names the act of stretching. |
| `torture_v` | torture (cause extreme pain to someone) | мучити | мучити | a → мучити | ok | Torturing a prisoner is мучити, imperfective, listed in Wiktionary; aspect partner измучити is the standard completed form. |
| `addiction_n` | addiction (strong need for a harmful substance) | зависност | зависност | b → зависност | ok | Both give зависност, the common word for alcohol addiction; b's gloss correctly states need for a harmful substance, not a mere habit. |
| `curve_n` | curve (line that is not straight) | кривина | кривина | b → кривина | ok | Both give кривина; b's gloss is the general not-straight line, while a restricts it to road bends. |
| `depressed_adj` | depressed (very sad and without hope) | депресиван | депримован | a → депресиван | ok | Standard adjective for feeling depressed after job loss; депримован is a rarer, more colloquial form. |
| `horn_n` | horn (device making loud sound in vehicles) | сирена | сирена | b → — | quarantined | Both give сирена; b's gloss, a device making loud sound in vehicles, matches the car horn, while a's 'sound signal' is vague. · judge: fail: Труба is a trumpet or pipe, not the usual word for a car horn; сирена or клаксон names the vehicle device, so the term is a different thing. |
| `inspection_n` | inspection (careful examination of something) | преглед | инспекција | a → преглед | ok | Преглед is the common native word for a safety or technical check; инспекција mainly names the official body or a formal audit. |
| `install_v` | install (put software or equipment in place) | инсталирати | инсталирати | b → инсталирати | ok | Инсталирати is the standard word and is used as biaspectual; answer b gives the right software-or-equipment gloss, while a's aspect partner is just a variant. |
| `instruction_n` | instruction (direction on how to do something) | упутство | упутство | b → упутство | ok | Both give упутство; b's gloss, advice on how to do something, matches the follow-the-instructions sense, while a's 'direction for work' is vaguer. |
| `invitation_n` | invitation (request to attend an event) | позив | позивница | b → позивница | ok | A wedding invitation is a written card; позивница names exactly that, while позив also means call or summons. |
| `lean_adj` | lean (having little fat on body or meat) | мршав | сув | a → мршав | ok | мршав covers a body or meat with little fat; сув means dry or gaunt and is not about fat. |
| `lean_v` | lean (rest against something for support) | наслањати | наслонити се | other → наслањати се | ok | Leaning against a wall is reflexive; А lacks се and B's наслонити се is perfective, so imperfective наслањати се is right. · judge: pass: наслањати се is the imperfective reflexive verb for leaning against something, common and exact. |
| `migration_n` | migration (movement of people or animals to a new place) | миграција | сеоба | b → сеоба | ok | Сеоба птица is the native idiom for bird migration and also covers peoples; миграција skews to human population movements. |
| `acquire_v` | acquire (to get or obtain something) | стицати | стечати | a → стицати | ok | Стицати is the real imperfective of стећи, fitting acquiring skills; стечати is not an established Serbian verb. |
| `provision_n` | provision (act of providing or supplying something needed) | обезбеђивање | снабдевање | b → снабдевање | collision | Provision of food is the act of supplying it, снабдевање храном; обезбеђивање rather means securing or guaranteeing. |
| `alike_adv` | alike (in a similar way) | слично | исто | a → слично | ok | Two children behaving alike means in a similar way, слично; исто means identically or also, stronger than the sense. |
| `flexible_adj` | flexible (able to bend easily without breaking) | савитљив | флексибилан | a → савитљив | ok | Physical bending of shoes calls for савитљив; флексибилан is mostly figurative, as in a flexible schedule. |
| `companion_n` | companion (person who accompanies another) | пратилац | сапутник | b → сапутник | ok | Сапутник is one who accompanies another on a path; пратилац leans toward escort or follower, as the back-translation shows. |
| `consultant_n` | consultant (expert hired to give professional advice) | консултант | саветник | a → консултант | ok | Консултант is the usual word for a hired external expert on an IT project; саветник suggests an in-house adviser or official. |
| `mere_adj` | mere (only or just used for emphasis) | пуки | само | a → пуки | ok | Пуки is the attributive adjective meaning mere, as in пука сугестија; само is an adverb and cannot modify a noun here. |
| `haven_n` | haven (safe place or shelter) | уточиште | склониште | a → уточиште | ok | Library as a calm refuge from noise needs a safe-place sense; уточиште fits, whereas склониште suggests a hideout or air-raid shelter. |
| `retreat_n` | retreat (a quiet or secluded place) | повлачење | летовалиште | other → уточиште | collision | Повлачење means military withdrawal and летовалиште a summer resort; уточиште is a quiet refuge, like a mountain hideaway. · judge: pass: уточиште is an established noun for a sheltered, peaceful refuge, fitting a mountain retreat acceptably. |
| `appreciation_n` | appreciation (understanding and enjoyment of quality) | уважавање | поштовање | b → — | quarantined | Deep appreciation for art is дубоко поштовање; уважавање is more formal and concerns esteem for persons or opinions. · judge: fail: Цењење mostly means valuing or esteeming and is a weak, rarely used verbal noun; it does not convey understanding and enjoyment of art, where поштовање or уважавање is closer. |
| `dive_v` | dive (jump or fall head first into water) | ронити | скочити | other → — | quarantined | Diving from a board is jumping into water; b is perfective скочити, so imperfective скакати is used, while ронити means going underwater. · judge: fail: Скакати means to jump or hop, and the head-first plunge into water is missing. The sense needs ронити (underwater) or скакати на главу, and the bare verb is too generic. |
| `dive_n` | dive (act of jumping into water head first) | роњење | скок | b → скок | collision | A perfect dive into the pool is a jump into water; роњење means underwater swimming or scuba diving. |
| `heal_v` | heal (become healthy again after injury or illness) | залечити | зарастати | b → зарастати | ok | A wound closing over and healing is зарастати, imperfective; залечити is perfective and 'постать здрав' is not a Serbian form. |
| `journalism_n` | journalism (the work of reporting news) | новинарство | новинарство | a → новинарство | ok | Новинарство is the common native noun for studying journalism; журналистика is the rarer loan variant used for the academic field. |
| `resume_v` | resume (start again after stopping) | наставити | наставити | other → настављати | collision | Both answers chose perfective наставити; the required imperfective infinitive is настављати, which suits resuming a meeting. · judge: pass: Настављати is a common imperfective verb, and настављати састанак fits resuming the meeting. |
| `ridge_n` | ridge (long raised line on land or back) | гребен | грб | a → гребен | ok | Walking along a mountain ridge is по гребену; грб means back or coat of arms and is not used for ridges. |
| `alternate_v` | alternate (take turns or switch regularly) | смењивати | измењивати се | other → смењивати се | ok | Taking turns on weekends is reflexive смењивати се; a drops се and so means to replace someone, while измењивати се is rarer. · judge: pass: смењивати се is the common imperfective reflexive verb for taking turns, fitting alternating weekend work. |
| `closure_n` | closure (feeling of resolution after an event) | затварање | задовољство | unsure → — | quarantined | Затварање means shutting and задовољство means satisfaction; Serbian has no established single word for emotional closure after an event. |
| `convert_v` | convert (change into a different form or belief) | претварати | претворити | a → претварати | ok | Imperfective with correct partner претворити; fits turning an attic into a bedroom, whereas b gives the perfective. |
| `defender_n` | defender (person who protects or argues for) | бранилац | бранитељ | a → бранилац | ok | Бранилац is the Serbian standard for defender, including of rights; бранитељ is the Croatian-leaning variant. |
| `editorial_n` | editorial (newspaper article expressing opinion) | уводник | уреднички чланак | a → уводник | ok | Уводник is the established single word for a newspaper opinion column; уреднички чланак is a descriptive paraphrase. |
| `grip_n` | grip (firm hold with the hand) | хват | ухват | a → хват | ok | Хват is the established term for a hand hold (sport, tools); ухват means a handhold or catch. |
| `grip_v` | grip (to hold something tightly) | чврсто држати | чврсто држати | other → стискати | ok | Both answers give a two-word phrase with odd aspect partners; imperfective стискати is one common word for gripping a steering wheel. · judge: pass: Стискати is an imperfective verb, and стискати волан fits holding the steering wheel tightly. |
| `improved_adj` | improved (better than before) | побољшан | бољи | a → побољшан | ok | The improved system is the participle побољшан, meaning made better; бољи is just the comparative of good and lacks the before-and-after sense. |
| `mild_adj` | mild (gentle or not extreme) | благ | благи | a → благ | ok | Благо време is the standard collocation for mild weather; indefinite masculine благ is the dictionary form, not definite благи. |
| `preference_n` | preference (liking one thing more than another) | преференција | преференција | a → преференција | ok | Both answers give the same word; it is an established noun for liking one thing over another, as in tea over coffee. |
| `booth_n` | booth (small enclosed space for selling or working) | тезга | киоск | a → тезга | collision | A fruit seller at a market works at a тезга; киоск is a freestanding street kiosk, a different thing. |
| `fatal_adj` | fatal (causing death or disaster) | смртоносан | фаталан | b → фаталан | ok | An accident fatal for a driver is фаталан, in common use for accidents; смртоносан suits poisons and weapons. |
| `declaration_n` | declaration (a formal public statement of intentions or facts) | декларација | изјава | a → декларација | ok | 'Declaration of independence' is a formal document, rendered Декларација о независности; изјава is merely a statement. |
| `lung_n` | lung (one of the two organs for breathing) | плућа | плуће | a → плућа | ok | Плућа is a plural-only noun and the standard form, matching the example's lungs; плуће is a rare singular. |
| `rental_n` | rental (payment for using property or vehicle) | најам | закуп | a → најам | ok | Payment for renting a vehicle or flat is најам; закуп is formal leasing, mostly of land or premises. |
| `intend_v` | intend (plan or aim to do something) | намеравати | намеравати | a → намеравати | ok | Both give the same imperfective; A's partner намерити is the proper pair, B's намерити се is a different verb. |
| `lawsuit_n` | lawsuit (legal case in a court of law) | парница | тужба | b → тужба | ok | Filing a lawsuit is поднети тужбу; тужба is far more common than the formal парница. |
| `leak_v` | leak (allow liquid or gas to escape) | цурити | цурити | a → цурити | ok | Imperfective цурити fits a pipe leaking water; A gives the correct partner процурити, while B's прцурити is wrong. |
| `slot_n` | slot (narrow opening or space for inserting something) | прорез | рези | a → прорез | ok | Letter slot is прорез, a real noun for a narrow opening; B рези is not an established word. |
| `smash_v` | smash (break something into pieces violently) | разбијати | разбити | a → разбијати | ok | B разбити is perfective; imperfective разбијати fits violently breaking a window into pieces. |
| `suspicious_adj` | suspicious (feeling or causing doubt about someone's honesty) | сумњив | подозрив | a → сумњив | ok | His behavior seemed suspicious describes behaviour causing doubt, which сумњив covers; подозрив means feeling suspicion oneself. |
| `traffic_jam_n` | traffic jam (long line of stationary vehicles) | застој | заглављење | a → застој | ok | Застој is the standard traffic-news term for a jam; заглављење is not an idiomatic noun for stuck vehicles. |
| `basin_n` | basin (shallow container for washing) | лавор | корито | a → лавор | ok | Лавор is the standard Serbian word for a washing basin; корито means a trough or riverbed, wrong for washing a face. |
| `beneficial_adj` | beneficial (resulting in good or helpful effects) | користан | користан | a → користан | collision | Користан is the common adjective for good effects; both agree, and a's general gloss is not limited to health. |
| `breed_v` | breed (produce offspring of animals) | гајити | размножавати | a → гајити | ok | Breeding horses on a farm is raising animals deliberately; гајити fits that, while размножавати describes the animals reproducing themselves. |
| `bud_n` | bud (small growth on a plant) | пупољак | пупољак | a → пупољак | ok | Both give пупољак, the standard word for an unopened rose bud; a's gloss of shoot is looser but the term fits. |
| `console_v` | console (to comfort someone sad) | тешити | утешити | a → тешити | ok | Imperfective тешити has утешити as partner; B утешити is perfective, flagged hard, so A is the right dictionary form. |
| `fabulous_adj` | fabulous (extremely good or impressive) | фабулозан | невероватан | other → сјајан | collision | Фабулозан is a rare slang borrowing and невероватан means unbelievable; сјајан is the common word for an excellent concert. · judge: pass: сјајан is a very common standard Serbian adjective for excellent, impressive, fitting the fabulous concert. |
| `fold_v` | fold (bend paper or cloth over) | савити | савити | other → пресавијати | ok | Both answers give perfective савити; folding a letter over is imperfective пресавијати, with пресавити as partner. · judge: pass: пресавијати is the established imperfective verb for folding paper or cloth, fits folding a letter. |
| `glorious_adj` | glorious (very beautiful or enjoyable) | диван | прекрасан | a → диван | collision | 'A glorious day at the beach' is a lovely, enjoyable day: диван дан; прекрасан is stronger on beauty alone. |
| `lodge_n` | lodge (a small house in the countryside or woods) | колиба | луг | a → колиба | collision | A wooden lodge in the countryside is a hut or cabin, колиба; луг means a grove or wood, not a building. |
| `rebel_n` | rebel (person who opposes authority or rules) | побуњеник | бунтовник | b → бунтовник | ok | A person defying a king's orders is a general bunt-maker, бунтовник; побуњеник suggests one who has risen in an uprising. |
| `rebel_v` | rebel (to oppose authority or rules) | бунити | бунити се | b → бунити се | ok | Rebelling against parents needs the reflexive бунити се; plain бунити means to rouse or upset someone. |
| `unbelievable_adj` | unbelievable (impossible to believe or very surprising) | невероватан | неверојатан | a → невероватан | collision | Невероватан has the highest frequency of the Ekavian forms and covers both disbelief and surprise. |
| `variation_n` | variation (a slightly different form or version) | варијанта | варијација | a → варијанта | ok | Recipe variations are slightly different versions of it, which варијанта covers; варијација is more musical or statistical. |
| `altogether_adv` | altogether (completely or totally) | потпуно | у потпуности | a → потпуно | ok | Altogether a mistake means completely a mistake; потпуно is a plain adverb here, so the adjective flag is wrong, and it is shorter than the phrase. |
| `drain_n` | drain (pipe for waste water) | одвод | одвод | other → сливник | ok | A blocked drain in sink or floor is сливник, the established noun; одвод means an outlet or diversion and is vaguer. · judge: pass: Сливник is the common Serbian noun for a drain opening, and сливник је запушен fits the blocked-drain example. |
| `drain_v` | drain (remove liquid from) | цедити | одренирати | a → цедити | ok | Draining pasta is цедити (partner оцедити); B одренирати is perfective and suited to land or wounds. |
| `excellence_n` | excellence (the quality of being very good or superior) | изврсност | изванредност | a → — | quarantined | Academic excellence is изврсност, an established noun; изванредност is rarer and sounds like extraordinariness. · judge: fail: Одличност is rare and mostly means being excellent as a grade; изврсност is the standard Serbian noun for academic excellence, so this term is not the common word. |
| `miserable_adj` | miserable (very unhappy or uncomfortable) | несрећан | мизеран | a → несрећан | collision | Несрећан is the common neutral word for feeling very unhappy; мизеран means wretched or paltry and is less natural for feelings. |
| `nationwide_adj` | nationwide (covering the whole country) | национални | националан | unsure → — | quarantined | Национални means national, not covering the whole country; Serbian normally uses the phrase широм земље, with no single adjective. |
| `outlook_n` | outlook (way of thinking about life) | поглед | поглед на свет | b → поглед на свет | ok | Alone, поглед means a look or view; поглед на свет specifically means a way of seeing life, as in the example. |
| `scan_n` | scan (image created by a scanner) | скенирање | скен | b → скен | ok | The sense is the image produced by a scanner; скенирање names the act of scanning, not the resulting document image. |
| `span_v` | span (last for a period of time) | трајати | обухватати | a → трајати | collision | A course spanning six months simply lasts that long; трајати is imperfective and matches the sense, unlike обухватати. |
| `supporter_n` | supporter (person who supports a team or cause) | присталица | навијач | b → навијач | ok | Loyal fans of a club are supporters in the sports sense; навијач names this exactly, присталица suits parties and causes. |
| `tremendous_adj` | tremendous (very great in size or amount) | огроман | импресиван | a → огроман | collision | Огроман means huge in size or degree and suits a tremendous job; импресиван means impressive, a different nuance. |
| `adorable_adj` | adorable (very cute or charming) | сладак | сладок | a → сладак | collision | Сладак is the Serbian form used colloquially for a cute baby; сладок is the Church Slavonic or Bulgarian-style variant. |
| `assure_v` | assure (tell someone positively to remove doubt) | уверавати | осигурати | a → уверавати | ok | Уверавати is imperfective and means to assure someone to remove doubt; осигурати is perfective and means to insure or secure. |
| `spectacular_adj` | spectacular (very impressive or beautiful) | спектакуларан | спектакларан | a → спектакуларан | ok | B спектакларан is a misspelling lacking the у; спектакуларан is the correct standard form. |
| `accomplish_v` | accomplish (to successfully complete a task) | остваривати | извршити | a → остваривати | collision | Imperfective with partner остварити, fitting reaching a goal; answer b извршити is perfective and means carry out an order. |
| `adequate_adj` | adequate (sufficient for a specific need or purpose) | адекватан | довољан | b → довољан | collision | Food supply is adequate means enough, which довољан states plainly; адекватан suggests fit or appropriate rather than quantity. |
| `alter_v` | alter (change something slightly) | мењати | промењивати | a → мењати | collision | Altering a plan slightly is мењати, a common imperfective; промењивати is rare (zipf 0) and means changing repeatedly. |
| `buzz_n` | buzz (continuous low humming sound) | зујање | зумбање | a → зујање | ok | Зујање is the native noun for bees' humming; зумбање is a rare variant and its gloss says loud. |
| `buzz_v` | buzz (make a low humming sound) | зујати | зумбати | a → зујати | ok | Imperfective native verb for bees buzzing; зумбати is a marginal dialect form. |
| `declare_v` | declare (to state something officially or clearly) | изјавити | објавити | other → проглашавати | ok | Both answers are perfective; 'declared the meeting open' is formally proclaiming, for which the imperfective проглашавати fits best. · judge: pass: Проглашавати is an imperfective verb, and прогласити састанак отвореним is the standard phrase for declaring a meeting open. |
| `dip_v` | dip (put something briefly into liquid) | умакати | потапати | a → умакати | ok | Умакати is imperfective and means dipping bread in soup; the perfective flag is wrong. Потапати implies full submersion. |
| `effectiveness_n` | effectiveness (quality of being successful) | ефикасност | ефикасност | b → ефикасност | collision | Both give the same word; ефикасност is the common noun for how well a drug works, so the term stands. |
| `foul_adj` | foul (very unpleasant or dirty) | одвратан | гадан | a → одвратан | collision | A foul smell is одвратан, strongly repulsive; гадан is weaker and more colloquial, also meaning nasty or mean. |
| `graph_n` | graph (a diagram showing measurements or data) | график | граф | a → график | ok | Chart showing a trend is график in everyday Serbian; граф is the mathematical network or a count (nobleman), wrong sense here. |
| `interact_v` | interact (communicate or work with others) | комуницирати | интераговати | b → интераговати | ok | Students interacting in groups means mutual action; комуницирати narrows it to communication, so интераговати fits the sense better. |
| `measurement_n` | measurement (act of measuring size or amount) | мерење | мерање | a → мерење | ok | Мерење is the standard verbal noun of мерити; мерање is not a normal Serbian form. |
| `stack_n` | stack (pile of items arranged on top of each other) | стог | стоп | other → гомила | collision | A stack of books is a pile; стог means haystack and стоп is not the word, so гомила is the neutral established noun. · judge: pass: гомила is the common noun for a pile of things, acceptable for a stack of books. |
| `supportive_adj` | supportive (giving help or encouragement) | подржавајући | подржавајућ | a → подржавајући | ok | Подржавајући is the usable participial adjective for a supportive person; B's form подржавајућ is not a valid dictionary form. |
| `withdraw_v` | withdraw (take money out of a bank account) | подизати | повући | a → подизати | collision | Подизати новац is the idiomatic imperfective for taking cash from an ATM; answer b повући is perfective. |
| `withdrawal_n` | withdrawal (act of taking money out of a bank) | подизање | повлачење | a → подизање | ok | Подизање новца is the banking expression for a cash withdrawal; повлачење is used for retreat or withdrawing a claim. |
| `airline_n` | airline (company that operates aircraft) | авиокомпанија | авио-компанија | a → авиокомпанија | ok | Common one-word form for an airline company; b's gloss describes a generic firm and its hyphenated spelling is less standard. |
| `backwards_adv` | backwards (in the direction behind or towards the rear) | уназад | назад | b → назад | collision | Car moving backwards is plain direction toward the rear; назад is the common neutral adverb, уназад is rarer and bookish. |
| `bolt_n` | bolt (metal pin used to fasten two pieces together) | вијак | вијак | a → вијак | collision | Both give вијак, the common neutral word for a threaded fastener tightened with a wrench. |
| `collar_n` | collar (part of a shirt around the neck) | крагна | овратник | a → крагна | ok | Крагна is the ordinary spoken word in Serbia for a shirt collar; овратник is rarer, more regional or technical. |
| `cope_v` | cope (deal successfully with a difficult situation) | носити се | суочавати се | a → носити се | collision | Coping with too much work is носити се с нечим, listed by Wiktionary; суочавати се means merely facing, without succeeding. |
| `creepy_adj` | creepy (causing an unpleasant feeling of fear) | језив | згуснут | a → језив | ok | Језив denotes eerie, spine-chilling things like an old house at night; згуснут means condensed, a wrong sense. |
| `immigrant_n` | immigrant (person who moves to a new country) | досељеник | досељеник | other → имигрант | ok | Both give досељеник, rare in frequency and also meaning internal settler; имигрант is the current common word for one moving abroad. · judge: pass: имигрант is a standard established noun in Serbian for a person moving to a new country. |
| `intermediate_adj` | intermediate (middle level of ability) | средњи | средњи | b → средњи | collision | Same word in both; B's gloss, middle level of ability, matches a learner who is neither beginner nor advanced. |
| `sketch_v` | sketch (draw a quick rough drawing) | скицирати | скакивати | a → — | quarantined | Скицирати is an imperfective -ирати verb meaning to sketch; скакивати means something else entirely (leaping). |
| `spite_n` | spite (desire to hurt or annoy someone) | инат | мржња | other → пакост | ok | Acting out of pure spite is из пакости; инат means stubbornness or defiance, and мржња is hatred, not wish to annoy. · judge: pass: пакост is the established noun for spite or malice, fitting doing something out of pure spite. |
| `strengthen_v` | strengthen (make stronger or more effective) | јачати | ојачати | a → јачати | ok | Imperfective verb as required; b ојачати is its perfective partner. Fits ongoing exercise strengthening muscles. |
| `sunset_n` | sunset (time when the sun goes down) | залазак сунца | залазак сунца | b → залазак сунца | ok | Same phrase from both; b's gloss correctly describes the moment of the sun going down, whereas a's 'evening twilight' is a different phase. |
| `transform_v` | transform (change completely in form or appearance) | преобразити | трансформисати | b → трансформисати | ok | Verbs in -исати are biaspectual, so it works as the infinitive; it is the common word for renovation changing a house completely. |
| `cease_v` | cease (stop doing something formally) | престајати | престати | a → престајати | collision | Imperfective required; престати is perfective with the flag naming престајати. Fits ceasing production. |
| `elect_v` | elect (choose someone for a public position) | бирати | изабрати | a → бирати | collision | Imperfective бирати with partner изабрати fits electing a mayor; b is perfective and not allowed as the term. |
| `heart_warming_adj` | heart-warming (making you feel happy and touched) | дирљив | топол | a → дирљив | collision | A story that touches the feelings is дирљив; топол (should be топао) describes temperature or personal warmth, not stories. |
| `ladder_n` | ladder (steps for climbing up or down) | мердевине | лествица | a → мердевине | ok | Plural-only мердевине is the everyday Serbian word for a climbing ladder; лествица is bookish or figurative and means a scale. |
| `lobby_n` | lobby (large entrance hall in a building) | предворје | лоби | a → предворје | ok | Hotel entrance hall is предворје in standard Serbian; лоби is a loanword with zero listed frequency and also suggests political lobbying. |
| `profitable_adj` | profitable (producing financial gain) | уносан | исплатив | a → уносан | ok | Уносан is the common word for a business yielding gain, higher in frequency; исплатив is rare and means worthwhile. |
| `supplement_n` | supplement (extra food or vitamin pill) | додатак | додатак исхрани | b → додатак исхрани | ok | Vitamin pill is the fixed expression додатак исхрани, while bare додатак is vague and means any addition or appendix. |
| `trunk_n` | trunk (main part of a tree) | стабло | дебло | b → дебло | ok | Дебло is the thick trunk proper of a tree; стабло denotes the whole stem or tree plant. |
| `anchor_n` | anchor (metal device to hold a ship in place) | сидро | котва | a → сидро | ok | A boat dropping its anchor in harbour is сидро, the standard Serbian word; котва is a Croatian or Slovak-type form. |
| `attached_adj` | attached (emotionally close) | везан | приљубљен | a → везан | ok | Везан за is the idiomatic phrase for being emotionally attached to a pet; приљубљен means pressed close physically. |
| `complexity_n` | complexity (quality of being complicated or difficult to understand) | сложеност | комплексност | a → сложеност | ok | Сложеност is the native noun for a plan being complicated; комплексност is a heavier foreign alternative. |
| `extract_v` | extract (to remove or take out something with effort) | вадити | извадити | a → вадити | ok | A is imperfective with извадити as partner; B is perfective, and вадити зуб is the usual phrase for pulling a tooth. |
| `extract_n` | extract (short passage taken from a book or speech) | одломак | извод | a → одломак | collision | Одломак из романа is the standard word for a passage from a novel; извод means a summary or official certificate. |
| `slam_v` | slam (to shut something with a loud noise) | лупати | заклопити | a → лупати | collision | Imperfective лупати with noise suits a slammed door; заклопити is perfective and means to cover or close a lid. |
| `keeper_n` | keeper (person who guards or looks after something) | чувар | чувар | b → чувар | collision | Same word; b's gloss, one who cares for something, suits a zoo keeper feeding animals better than a's guarding sense. |
| `utterly_adv` | utterly (completely or absolutely) | потпуно | потпуно | a → потпуно | quarantined | Both give потпуно; it is a regular adverb here, so the adjective flag is wrong, and it fits utterly shocking. |
| `mansion_n` | mansion (very large and impressive house) | вила | палата | a → вила | collision | Вила is the common word for a large luxurious private house; палата suggests a palace or big public building. |
| `shipping_n` | shipping (transport of goods by sea) | отпрема | превоз | b → превоз | collision | Shipping costs are the cost of transporting goods; превоз is the general freight term, while отпрема means dispatching. |
| `evaluate_v` | evaluate (judge the quality or value of something) | оцењивати | процењивати | a → оцењивати | ok | Teachers assessing student performance is grading, which оцењивати expresses directly; both are imperfective but this fits better. |
| `pad_n` | pad (soft cushion or protective layer) | јастук | јастучић | b → јастучић | ok | Foam pad under a box is a small protective cushion; јастук means a bed pillow, wrong sense. Gender is masculine though. |
| `sail_v` | sail (travel on water using wind power) | једрити | пловити | a → једрити | ok | Wind-powered sailing across a lake is specifically једрити; пловити means any travel on water, including motor boats. |
| `tan_n` | tan (brown skin colour from sun exposure) | препланулост | загорелост | a → препланулост | ok | Препланулост is the established Serbian noun for sun-browned skin; загорелост is a Russian-style form not natural in Serbia. |
| `transparent_adj` | transparent (allowing light to pass through clearly) | провидан | прозиран | b → прозиран | ok | Прозиран is the adjective Wiktionary matches to this sense, used for see-through glass; провидан is also valid but often figurative. |
| `whip_v` | whip (beat ingredients until light and fluffy) | мутити | мути | a → мутити | collision | Мутити is the imperfective infinitive for whipping cream; b's мути is not an infinitive form. |
| `acute_adj` | acute (very severe or serious) | акутан | оштар | a → акутан | ok | Акутан бол is the standard collocation for sudden severe pain; оштар means sharp and covers cutting edges or tone. |
| `dash_n` | dash (small amount of something) | прстохват | прста | a → прстохват | ok | Прстохват is an established word for a small pinch of salt or spice; прста is not a noun for this meaning. |
| `dash_v` | dash (run somewhere very quickly) | јурити | трчати | a → јурити | collision | Јурити carries the sudden rush of dashing out; plain трчати lacks the speed, and the answer has a correct aspect partner. |
| `disposal_n` | disposal (the act of getting rid of something) | одлагање | отпадна вода | a → одлагање | ok | Одлагање отпада is the legal phrase for waste disposal; b's отпадна вода means wastewater, a different thing. |
| `global_warming_n` | global warming (gradual increase in Earth's temperature) | глобално загревање | глобално затопљење | a → глобално загревање | ok | Глобално загревање is the Serbian-standard term; затопљење is the Croatian-leaning variant. |
| `outlet_n` | outlet (socket for electrical plugs) | утичница | утичница | a → утичница | ok | Утичница is the standard Serbian word for a wall socket; both answers agree and fit plugging in a lamp. |
| `publicity_n` | publicity (attention from the media) | публицитет | јавност | a → публицитет | ok | Публицитет names media attention such as bad publicity after a scandal; јавност means the public. |
| `summit_n_meeting_between` | summit (meeting between leaders) | самит | самит | a → самит | ok | Both give самит, the established word for a meeting between leaders; a's gloss 'meeting at the top' fits the example. |
| `facilitate_v` | facilitate (make a process easier) | олакшавати | олакшати | a → олакшавати | ok | Imperfective олакшавати with partner олакшати fits facilitating communication; b gives the perfective олакшати, which is disallowed. |
| `clause_n` | clause (part of a sentence) | клаузула | реченица | a → — | quarantined | Reading the first clause carefully points to a numbered clause, which клаузула names; реченица means a whole sentence, not a part. · term override: клаузула is a contract or legal clause, not a grammatical one (the T3 audit); Serbian grammar names it by kind (независна, зависна реченица), with no single established word |
| `dock_n` | dock (area where ships load or unload) | док | доков | a → док | ok | A ship arriving at the dock is док; B доков is an inflected or invented form, not a nominative noun. |
| `sneak_v` | sneak (move quietly to avoid being seen) | шуњати се | красти се | a → шуњати се | ok | Sneaking out of a room unseen is шуњати се, the usual imperfective; красти се is archaic or poetic. |
| `infant_n` | infant (very young baby) | одојче | беба | b → беба | collision | Беба is the common neutral word for a very young baby; одојче is a nursing term, also meaning suckling piglet. |
| `furious_adj` | furious (very angry) | бесан | љутит | a → бесан | ok | Furious about the delay is intense rage; бесан is the common Serbian adjective, while љутит is rarer and weaker, meaning hot-tempered. |
| `jaw_n` | jaw (bone structure holding teeth) | вилица | вилица | a → — | quarantined | Вилица is the ordinary Serbian word for the jaw bone; the Croatian-word flag is wrong since виљушка is the fork. |
| `sensible_adj` | sensible (showing good judgment and practicality) | разуман | смислен | a → разуман | collision | Warm clothes as a sensible choice is разуман избор; смислен means meaningful, not practical good judgment. |
| `resign_v` | resign (quit a job or position) | дати оставку | отказати се | other → подносити оставку | ok | Both answers are perfective (дати оставку, отказати се); подносити оставку is the imperfective standard expression for resigning from a post. · judge: pass: Подносити оставку is the standard fixed expression for resigning from a post, and Serbian has no single common verb for it. |
| `merger_n` | merger (joining of two companies) | спајање | спој | a → спајање | ok | Joining of two firms is спајање компанија, the usual business term; спој is more a physical junction or joint. |
| `slap_n` | slap (hit with open hand) | шамар | плесак | a → шамар | ok | Шамар is the common word for a slap on the face; плесак is regional and rarer. |
| `superintendent_n` | superintendent (person in charge of police or schools) | надзорник | начелник | b → начелник | ok | Head of a police department is начелник; надзорник means overseer or supervisor and is not the established title for police or school heads. |
| `slap_v` | slap (hit with open hand) | шамарати | плеснути | a → шамарати | ok | B плеснути is perfective; шамарати is imperfective and specifically means hitting someone on the face, as in the example. |
| `sweep_v` | sweep (clean floor with broom) | мести | мети | a → мести | ok | Мести is the correct imperfective infinitive of sweep, with partner помести; мети is not a valid Serbian infinitive. |
| `thereafter_adv` | thereafter (from that time on) | после тога | након тога | a → после тога | ok | For 'from that time on' the common neutral phrase is после тога; након тога is more formal and bookish. |
| `tuition_n` | tuition (fee for teaching or instruction) | школарина | настава | a → школарина | ok | Tuition fees are the payment itself; школарина is the established word for it, whereas настава means teaching. |
| `divided_adj` | divided (disagreeing or split into groups) | подељен | подељен | b → подељен | ok | Same word in both; committee divided on an issue means split in opinion, which b's gloss states directly. |
| `pit_n_stone_inside` | pit (stone inside a fruit) | коштица | којица | a → коштица | ok | Коштица is the standard word for a fruit stone like a peach pit; којица is not a Serbian word. |
| `employ_v` | employ (give work to someone for pay) | запошљавати | запослити | a → запошљавати | ok | Company employs fifty people is a state of employing; imperfective запошљавати with partner запослити, whereas B is perfective. |
| `pirate_n` | pirate (person who attacks ships at sea) | пират | гусар | a → пират | ok | Пират is the neutral modern word for a sea attacker; гусар is dated, historical and mostly literary. |
| `revelation_n` | revelation (surprising new fact or truth) | откровење | откривење | a → откровење | ok | Откровење is a startling disclosure or truth, as in a shocking revelation; откривење leans toward discovery or the biblical term. |
| `superb_adj` | superb (excellent or very good) | изванредан | одличан | a → изванредан | collision | Superb food is more than just good; изванредан expresses outstanding quality, while одличан is the plainer excellent. |
| `spa_n` | spa (place for health treatments and relaxation) | бања | спа центар | b → спа центар | ok | A luxury spa for treatments is спа центар; бања means a thermal resort town or bath. |
| `trek_n` | trek (long difficult journey on foot) | поход | планинарење | a → поход | ok | Поход covers a long walking expedition such as a five-day trek; планинарење names the hobby of hiking in general. |
| `tricky_adj` | tricky (difficult or requiring care) | замршен | замршен | other → незгодан | collision | Незгодно питање is the natural phrase for a tricky question; замршен means tangled or intricate, not delicate or requiring care. · judge: pass: незгодан is a common adjective for awkward, delicate matters, fitting 'tricky question'; back-translation 'inconvenient' is only a nuance. |
| `waist_n` | waist (part of body between ribs and hips) | струка | појас | other → струк | ok | Serbian dictionaries list the waist as струк (masculine); струка means profession and појас is a belt or zone, so neither answer is exact. · judge: pass: струк is the standard everyday Serbian noun for waist, nominative singular, exact sense. |
| `wit_n` | wit (ability to say funny things) | духовитост | дотераност | a → духовитост | ok | Духовитост is the established noun for wit and humor; дотераност means smartness or polish in dress. |
| `compassion_n` | compassion (sympathy for others' suffering) | саосећање | саосећање | b → саосећање | ok | Both give саосећање; b's gloss states sympathy for others' suffering, while a's сажаљење drifts toward condescending pity. |
| `execute_v` | execute (to carry out a plan or task) | извршавати | извршити | a → извршавати | ok | Imperfective извршавати fits carrying out a project; B извршити is perfective and belongs in aspect_partner. |
| `philosophical_adj` | philosophical (accepting unpleasant situations calmly) | филозофски | философски | a → стоички | ok | Serbian standard spelling is филозофски with з; философски follows Russian and is not the Serbian norm. · judge: pass: Стоички is an established adjective for calm acceptance of misfortune, matching staying philosophical about bad news. |
| `scheduled_adj` | scheduled (planned for a specific time) | заказан | предвиђен | b → предвиђен | ok | Train is scheduled to arrive fits предвиђен (foreseen by the timetable); заказан suits booked appointments, not timetabled arrivals. |
| `squeeze_v` | squeeze (press something firmly from all sides) | цедити | стискати | a → цедити | collision | Squeezing a lemon into juice is extracting liquid, цедити; стискати only means pressing or clenching. |
| `threaten_v` | threaten (say you will harm someone) | претити | претити | a → претити | ok | Претити is actually imperfective with partner запретити, so the flag is wrong; gloss 'изрицати претњу' matches threatening. |
| `unconscious_adj` | unconscious (not awake or aware of surroundings) | без свести | несвестан | a → без свести | ok | After the fall he lost consciousness, which is без свести; несвестан means unaware of something, not knocked out. |
| `battlefield_n` | battlefield (place where a battle takes place) | бојно поље | бојиште | b → бојиште | ok | Single established noun for the place of battle; бојно поље is a two-word equivalent, so the one-word form is preferred. |
| `revival_n` | revival (act of becoming popular or active again) | препород | обнова | other → оживљавање | ok | A revival of interest in jazz is renewed liveliness, оживљавање; препород means rebirth of a nation or culture, and обнова means restoration. · judge: pass: оживљавање is an established noun for reviving, including interest or activity, fitting a revival of interest in jazz. |
| `soundtrack_n` | soundtrack (music recorded for a film or play) | звучна подлога | музика из филма | other → саундтрек | ok | Serbian uses the loan саундтрек for a film's recorded music; звучна подлога is a calque and музика из филма is a phrase. · judge: pass: саундтрек is the established loanword in Serbian for film music, fitting the movie example. |
| `competent_adj` | competent (having necessary skills) | способан | компетентан | b → компетентан | ok | A competent manager is компетентан, meaning qualified for the role; способан means capable or able in general. |
| `exceed_v` | exceed (go beyond a limit) | прекорачивати | премашивати | a → прекорачивати | ok | Exceeding a speed limit is the standard collocation прекорачити ограничење; the imperfective прекорачивати is the more common choice. |
| `hail_v` | hail (call out to attract attention) | дозивати | зазивати | a → дозивати | ok | Hailing a taxi is calling it over with the voice; дозивати means calling someone to come, while зазивати means invoking. |
| `hunting_n` | hunting (activity of chasing animals for food or sport) | лов | лоб | a → лов | collision | Лов is the standard noun for hunting; лоб is not a word here, it means forehead. |
| `optional_adj` | optional (not required or compulsory) | изборни | изборан | other → необавезан | ok | Изборни means elective and изборан means choosy; necessary 'not compulsory' for attendance is expressed by the established необавезан. · judge: pass: необавезан is the standard adjective for optional, masculine nominative, fits optional attendance. |
| `necessity_n` | necessity (something absolutely required or essential) | потреба | неопходност | a → потреба | collision | 'Basic necessity for life' is основна потреба, the common everyday word; неопходност is abstract, not a thing needed. |
| `offshore_adj` | offshore (located in the sea near land) | приобални | офшорни | b → — | quarantined | Offshore wind farms stand out at sea; приобални means coastal, along the shore, so офшорни fits better as an established loan. · judge: fail: Приобални means coastal, lying along the shore, while offshore means out at sea; the sense differs, and офшор is the term used for wind farms. |
| `verbal_adj` | verbal (expressed in words) | усмени | вербални | a → усмени | collision | A verbal confirmation means spoken, not written; усмени states that contrast, whereas вербални is more abstract or linguistic. |
| `abandoned_adj` | abandoned (left empty and not used or cared for) | напуштен | запуштен | a → напуштен | ok | Напуштен means left empty and deserted, as an abandoned house; запуштен means neglected or run-down, which is a different emphasis. |
| `convinced_adj` | convinced (certain that something is true or real) | уверен | убеђен | b → убеђен | ok | I am convinced that he tells the truth is a held conviction, the participle убеђен; уверен is closer to sure. |
| `rack_n` | rack (frame for holding things) | полица | рака | other → сталак | ok | Полица is a fixed shelf and рака means a tomb or shrine; сталак is the established word for a stand or rack holding plates. · judge: pass: сталак is the common noun for a stand or rack holding objects, fitting a dish drying rack. |
| `stiff_adj` | stiff (hard to bend or move) | укрућен | укочен | b → укочен | ok | Укочен is the usual word for a stiff neck or body part; укрућен means hardened or starched. |
| `browser_n` | browser (program to view web pages) | прегледач | претраживач | a → прегледач | ok | Прегледач is the standard Serbian term for a web browser; претраживач usually means a search engine. |
| `recruit_v` | recruit (hire new staff or soldiers) | регрутовати | запослити | other → запошљавати | collision | Example is a company hiring staff; запошљавати is the neutral imperfective for hiring, while регрутовати is mainly military and запослити is perfective. · judge: pass: Запошљавати is a common imperfective verb for hiring staff, and it fits a company taking on five people. |
| `midst_n` | midst (middle of a group or activity) | средина | средина | b → средина | collision | Среди гомиле, in the middle of the crowd, uses средина as centre of a group; a's gloss wrongly says inner part. |
| `nursery_n` | nursery (room for a young child) | дечја соба | дечија соба | a → дечја соба | ok | Дечја соба is the standard Serbian adjective form for a child's room; both answers name the same meaning with minor variant spelling. |
| `supernatural_adj` | supernatural (beyond natural laws or explanation) | натприродан | напредприродан | a → натприродан | ok | Supernatural elements in a story: натприродан is the dictionary adjective; напредприродан is not an established word. |
| `specialty_n` | speciality (special area of knowledge or skill) | специјалност | струка | a → специјалност | ok | His area of expertise is специјалност; струка means profession or trade, which shifts the meaning from skill area. |
| `fire_brigade_n` | fire brigade (organization that fights fires) | ватрогасци | ватрогасна служба | b → ватрогасна служба | ok | The sense is the organization, matched by ватрогасна служба; ватрогасци are the individual firefighters and plural-only. |
| `undoubtedly_adv` | undoubtedly (without any doubt at all) | несумњиво | непобитно | a → несумњиво | ok | Undoubtedly the best player is plain без сумње, rendered by несумњиво; непобитно means irrefutably and fits only proven claims. |
| `ranger_n` | ranger (person who protects a park or forest) | чувар | чувар природе | b → чувар природе | ok | Covers both park and forest protection in the example; plain чувар is too vague, meaning any guard or keeper. |
| `bully_v` | bully (treat someone cruelly to get what you want) | малтретирати | забуњивати | a → малтретирати | ok | Малтретирати is the established imperfective verb for bullying a weaker student; забуњивати means to confuse, a wrong sense. |
| `backyard_n` | backyard (garden area behind a house) | двориште | двориште | a → двориште | collision | Same word in both; двориште is the established Serbian word for yard behind a house, and a's gloss captures the garden sense. |
| `consult_v` | consult (to seek advice from an expert) | консултовати | консултовати се | a → консултовати | ok | Консултовати лекара takes a direct object, matching consult a doctor; the reflexive form in B needs a different construction. |
| `dominate_v` | dominate (be more important or powerful than others) | доминирати | доминовати | a → доминирати | ok | Company dominating a market is доминирати, imperfective and standard; B доминовати is a non-standard form. |
| `unacceptable_adj` | unacceptable (not allowed or not good enough) | неприхватљив | неприхватљив | b → неприхватљив | ok | Same word in both; the gloss 'not allowed' matches rudeness being forbidden, better than 'not tolerated'. |
| `zip_n` | zip (fastener on clothing) | рајсфершлус | рајзфершлус | a → рајсфершлус | ok | Рајсфершлус follows the usual Serbian spelling of the German loan Reissverschluss; the variant рајзфершлус is a misspelling of the same word. |
| `uncommon_adj` | uncommon (not happening often) | редак | неуобичајен | b → неуобичајен | collision | Snow being uncommon here means not customary or usual; редак means scarce or sparse, better for objects than for events. |
| `cooperative_adj` | cooperative (willing to help others) | сарадљив | попустљив | a → сарадљив | ok | Cooperative during a game means willing to work with others, which сарадљив states; попустљив means yielding or lenient. |
| `fulfil_v` | fulfil (achieve a goal or promise) | испуњавати | испунити | a → испуњавати | ok | Imperfective with partner испунити; answer b is perfective, and испуњавати fits fulfilling a dream. |
| `prompt_n` | prompt (a cue to remember or act) | подсетник | подсетник | a → подсетник | collision | Подсетник is an Ekavian established noun for a reminder, matching a cue to remember; both answers agree. |
| `prompt_v` | prompt (to cause or encourage action) | подстаћи | потицати | other → подстицати | collision | Подстаћи is perfective, and потицати in Serbia means to originate; подстицати is the imperfective for an event prompting a debate. · judge: pass: подстицати is a common imperfective verb for encouraging or triggering action, fitting an event prompting debate. |
| `clarify_v` | clarify (to make a statement clearer or easier to understand) | разјаснити | појаснити | other → разјашњавати | ok | Both answers are perfective; the imperfective разјашњавати means to clarify a point, as in the example. · judge: pass: разјашњавати is the standard imperfective verb for clarifying a point, with perfective разјаснити. |
| `correspondence_n` | correspondence (letters or emails exchanged) | преписка | кореспонденција | a → преписка | ok | Keeping up correspondence for years is a mutual exchange of letters, which преписка names; кореспонденција is more official mail. |
| `porch_n` | porch (covered shelter at the entrance of a building) | трем | тераса | a → трем | ok | Front porch is a covered veranda at the entrance, which трем names; тераса is an open terrace, usually above or behind. |
| `mock_adj` | mock (imitating someone to make fun of them) | подругљиви | подругљив | b → подругљив | ok | Dictionary lists the indefinite masculine form подругљив for mocking; a's definite form подругљиви is not the required lemma. |
| `utter_v` | utter (to say or speak words) | изговарати | изговорити | a → изговарати | collision | Not uttering a word is the imperfective изговарати with partner изговорити; B lists the perfective as term, which is barred. |
| `bracket_n` | bracket (punctuation mark ( ) or [ ]) | заграда | заграда | b → заграда | ok | Same word; B's gloss names the actual sense, enclosing text in brackets, while A's gloss is only generic punctuation. |
| `outrage_n` | outrage (strong anger or shock) | бес | негодовање | b → негодовање | ok | Public outrage at news is indignation, which негодовање names; бес is raw rage and does not fit shock. |
| `testify_v` | testify (give formal evidence in court) | сведочити | свједочити | a → сведочити | collision | Ekavian сведочити is the Serbian form and imperfective; свједочити in b is Ijekavian. |
| `tolerate_v` | tolerate (allow or endure something unpleasant) | трпети | подносити | a → трпети | ok | Не могу да трпим груби однос: трпети is imperfective and covers both allowing and enduring; подносити leans toward bearing a burden. |
| `exploit_v` | exploit (use unfairly for personal gain) | искоришћавати | искористити | a → искоришћавати | ok | Exploiting workers for cheap labour is a continuing unfair use; imperfective искоришћавати fits, B is perfective. |
| `imply_v` | imply (suggest without saying directly) | наговештавати | сугерисати | a → наговештавати | ok | Imply in 'are you implying' means to hint indirectly, наговештавати, imperfective; сугерисати means propose or suggest an idea. |
| `investigator_n` | investigator (person who conducts an official inquiry) | истражилац | истражитељ | b → истражитељ | ok | Official inquiry conducted by a person is истражитељ, a legal and police title; истражилац is a rare variant. |
| `naughty_adj` | naughty (mischievously disobedient or bad) | неваљао | непокоран | a → неваљао | ok | Standard word for a mischievous misbehaving child; непокоран means rebellious, not playful. |
| `obey_v` | obey (follow rules or orders) | слушати | поштовати | b → поштовати | collision | Traffic laws are complied with, not listened to; слушати suits persons, so поштовати (imperfective) fits following rules. |
| `overly_adv` | overly (to an excessive degree) | претерано | превише | b → превише | collision | Превише is the common neutral word for 'too much' before an adjective; претерано is rarer and bookish. |
| `straightforward_adj` | straightforward (easy to understand or do) | једноставан | јасан | a → једноставан | collision | Instructions that are easy to follow are једноставан; јасан means clear, which loses the easy-to-do side. |
| `thoughtful_adj` | thoughtful (kind and thinking of others) | пажљив | позабочен | a → пажљив | collision | Helping out as a kind, considerate gesture is пажљив; позабочен is not a standard Serbian word. |
| `confess_v` | confess (admit to doing something wrong) | признавати | признати | a → признавати | collision | B признати is perfective, flagged hard; A is the imperfective for admitting a mistake. |
