## Quarantined (never shown)

| key | English (sense) | best guess | reasons |
|---|---|---|---|
| `long_distance_adj` | long-distance (covering a great distance) | међуградски | judge_other: Међуградски means intercity, which is a different notion because an intercity route can be short. Long-distance, covering a great distance, would need дугопругашки or a similar word. · adjudicator: далекосежни means far-reaching in effect and дугачки means physically long; међуградски is the established adjective for long-distance buses and calls. |
| `mess_v` | mess (to make something dirty or untidy) | нередити | judge_other: Нередити is not an established standard verb; Serbian says правити неред or мешати, so this looks coined and is not common. · adjudicator: Imperfective and established; covers making a room dirty, though it only partly captures untidiness, while загадити is perfective and means pollute. |
| `burn_v` | burn (to be damaged by fire or heat) | опекати | perfective verb (local model); judge_other: опекати means to scorch or sear by contact and is flagged aspectually unreliable; the common verb for being burned is опећи се or горети. · adjudicator: Not burning your hand is injuring skin by heat, опекати; both answers are perfective (опећи, спалити), and спалити means burning something down. |
| `snap_v` | snap (take a quick photograph) | сликати | perfective verb (DeepSeek and local model) · adjudicator: Both are perfective; сликати is the common imperfective for taking a photo, as in Можеш ли да нас сликаш. |
| `dating_n` | dating (going on romantic dates with someone) | изласци | adjudicator unsure · adjudicator: Изласци only means outings, заљубљивање means falling in love; no established noun clearly means dating, the usual loan being дејтинг. |
| `horn_n` | horn (device making loud sound in vehicles) | труба | judge_other: Труба is a trumpet or pipe, not the usual word for a car horn; сирена or клаксон names the vehicle device, so the term is a different thing. · adjudicator: Both give сирена; b's gloss, a device making loud sound in vehicles, matches the car horn, while a's 'sound signal' is vague. |
| `appreciation_n` | appreciation (understanding and enjoyment of quality) | цењење | judge_other: Цењење mostly means valuing or esteeming and is a weak, rarely used verbal noun; it does not convey understanding and enjoyment of art, where поштовање or уважавање is closer. · adjudicator: Deep appreciation for art is дубоко поштовање; уважавање is more formal and concerns esteem for persons or opinions. |
| `dive_v` | dive (jump or fall head first into water) | скакати | judge_other: Скакати means to jump or hop, and the head-first plunge into water is missing. The sense needs ронити (underwater) or скакати на главу, and the bare verb is too generic. · adjudicator: Diving from a board is jumping into water; b is perfective скочити, so imperfective скакати is used, while ронити means going underwater. |
| `closure_n` | closure (feeling of resolution after an event) | затварање | adjudicator unsure · adjudicator: Затварање means shutting and задовољство means satisfaction; Serbian has no established single word for emotional closure after an event. |
| `excellence_n` | excellence (the quality of being very good or superior) | одличност | judge_other: Одличност is rare and mostly means being excellent as a grade; изврсност is the standard Serbian noun for academic excellence, so this term is not the common word. · adjudicator: Academic excellence is изврсност, an established noun; изванредност is rarer and sounds like extraordinariness. |
| `nationwide_adj` | nationwide (covering the whole country) | национални | adjudicator unsure · adjudicator: Национални means national, not covering the whole country; Serbian normally uses the phrase широм земље, with no single adjective. |
| `thesis_n` | thesis (long essay written for a university degree) | дисертација | judge_other: Дисертација means a doctoral dissertation, too narrow for a master's thesis; магистарски рад or теза would match the example. |
| `sketch_v` | sketch (draw a quick rough drawing) | скицирати | perfective verb (DeepSeek and local model) · adjudicator: Скицирати is an imperfective -ирати verb meaning to sketch; скакивати means something else entirely (leaping). |
| `clause_n` | clause (part of a sentence) | клаузула | override: клаузула is a contract or legal clause, not a grammatical one (the T3 audit); Serbian grammar names it by kind (независна, зависна реченица), with no single established word · adjudicator: Reading the first clause carefully points to a numbered clause, which клаузула names; реченица means a whole sentence, not a part. |
| `jaw_n` | jaw (bone structure holding teeth) | вилица | Croatian word; Serbian виљушка · adjudicator: Вилица is the ordinary Serbian word for the jaw bone; the Croatian-word flag is wrong since виљушка is the fork. |
| `offshore_adj` | offshore (located in the sea near land) | приобални | judge_other: Приобални means coastal, lying along the shore, while offshore means out at sea; the sense differs, and офшор is the term used for wind farms. · adjudicator: Offshore wind farms stand out at sea; приобални means coastal, along the shore, so офшорни fits better as an established loan. |
| `scent_n` | scent (smell, especially a pleasant one) | мирис | no reliable hint (the review found its hint wrong; no new one passed) |

## Duplicate synonyms in Serbian (never shown)

Another meaning has the same term in this language and no hint tells the two apart, so only that one keeps it. Not a translation failure.

| key | English (sense) | term | kept by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `budget_adj` | budget (low cost and inexpensive) | јефтин | `cheap_adj` (low in price) |
| `dumb_adj` | dumb (lacking intelligence or common sense) | глуп | `stupid_adj` (lacking intelligence or common sense) |
| `substitute_n` | substitute (person or thing replacing another) | замена | `replacement_n` (person or thing that takes the place of another) |
| `harbour_n` | harbour (place where ships dock) | лука | `port_n` (a place where ships load and unload cargo) |
| `utterly_adv` | utterly (completely or absolutely) | потпуно | `altogether_adv` (completely or totally) |

## Review: wrong and doubtful

| key | term | status | review |
|---|---|---|---|
| `spell_v` | изговарати слово по слово | ok | wrong: Срицати means to read or sound out slowly by syllables, not to spell a name aloud; the sense is wrong. (better: изговарати слово по слово) |
| `data_n` | подаци | ok | doubtful: Подаци is used as a plural-only noun, so the note should say g: pl; the missing note misleads learners. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; plural-only not confirmed: no g: pl) |
| `process_v` | обрађивати | ok | doubtful: Обрађивати with pf обрадити is correct for data, but the hint руководити подацима means managing and is misleading. |
| `project_v` | пројектовати | ok | doubtful: Пројектовати mainly means to design; for forecasting sales, прогнозирати is clearer and more usual. (better: прогнозирати) |
| `sound_adj` | поуздан | collision | doubtful: Поуздан means dependable; sound advice is well-founded and sensible, so разуман or здрав fits better. (better: разуман) |
| `chief_n` | шеф | collision | doubtful: Шеф means boss or manager; chief as leader of a group or tribe is closer to поглавар or поглавица. (better: поглавар) |
| `father_in_law_n` | свекар | ok | wrong: Свекар is only the husband's father (wife's father is таст); the sense says spouse's, and hint 'мужјин' is a non-word for 'мужев'. (better: таст) |
| `mother_in_law_n` | свекрва | ok | doubtful: Свекрва is only the husband's mother (ташта is the wife's); the hint also has non-word мужјина instead of мужева. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: мужева мајка) |
| `hard_drive_n` | тврди диск | ok | doubtful: Тврди диск is a literal calque; Serbian speakers overwhelmingly say хард диск in everyday use. (better: хард диск) |
| `brother_in_law_n` | зет | collision | wrong: Зет is a sister's or daughter's husband; for a spouse's brother Serbian uses шурак or девер, and the hint is garbled. (better: шурак) |
| `fear_v` | бојати се | ok | doubtful: Бојати се is correct and imperfective, but the hint 'осећавати' is not a real word (осећати), and no pf note is given. |
| `last_minute_adj` | последњег тренутка | ok | doubtful: Genitive phrase is not an adjective; a natural adjectival rendering is 'у последњем тренутку' or 'у последњи час'. (better: у последњем тренутку) |
| `stock_n` | бујон | ok | doubtful: Бујон mostly means bouillon or cubes; темељац or основа за супу names cooking stock more precisely. (better: темељац) |
| `commercial_n` | реклама | collision | doubtful: Term is right, but the hint 'кратка телевизијска оглас' has a gender mismatch; it should be кратак телевизијски оглас. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: кратак телевизијски оглас) |
| `lack_v` | недостајати | collision | doubtful: Недостајати has the reversed construction (нама недостаје), so it does not map to We lack skills directly. (better: оскудевати) |
| `grand_adj` | величанствен | ok | doubtful: Величанствен means majestic or magnificent; for a grand hotel раскошан or велелепан is more natural. (better: раскошан) |
| `challenge_v` | изазивати | collision | doubtful: Изазивати mainly means provoke or cause; for an exam testing someone's ability стављати на пробу is more natural, and the hint is vague. (better: стављати на пробу) |
| `comment_v` | коментарисати | ok | doubtful: Term is correct, but the note omits the perfective partner прокоментарисати that other verbs in this list carry. (better: коментарисати) |
| `executive_adj` | извршни | ok | doubtful: Извршни fits executive branch or извршни директор; for an 'executive position' руководећи (положај) is more natural. (better: руководећи) |
| `executive_n` | директор | collision | doubtful: Директор is narrower than 'executive'; the hint itself says руководилац, which is the more general fitting term. (better: руководилац) |
| `actual_adj` | стваран | collision | wrong: Стварни is the definite form; the convention requires the dictionary indefinite masculine form стваран. (better: стваран) |
| `division_n` | подела | ok | doubtful: Term подела is right, but hint раздељавање is not a normal Serbian word; it would mislead learners. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: раздељивање на делове) |
| `fat_n` | маст | ok | doubtful: Маст mostly means lard or animal fat; for dietary fat in 'too much fat' масноћа is the more natural word. (better: масноћа) |
| `benefit_v` | користити | collision | doubtful: Користити mainly means to use; in the benefit sense it needs a dative and is ambiguous for learners, and no pf is given. (better: бити од користи) |
| `living_n` | средства за живот | ok | wrong: Живот means life, not livelihood; make a living is зарађивати за живот, so a bare живот misleads. (better: средства за живот) |
| `cast_v` | додељивати улогу | ok | doubtful: Додељивати улогу is a descriptive phrase, not a fixed verb, and the note omits the perfective доделити; поделити улоге is the more natural collocation. (better: поделити улоге) |
| `criminal_adj` | криминалан | ok | wrong: Криминални is the definite form; the dictionary masculine form is криминалан (or кривични for a criminal record). (better: криминалан) |
| `real_estate_n` | некретнина | collision | doubtful: Real estate is collective; Serbian usually uses plural некретнине (invest in real estate), while singular некретнина means one property. (better: некретнине) |
| `reference_n` | помињање | ok | doubtful: Помињање is an awkward verbal noun for the mention in the example; помен is more natural, and the hint uses variant спомињање. (better: помен) |
| `stress_v` | наглашавати | ok | doubtful: Наглашавати is correct for stressing importance, but the note lacks the perfective partner нагласити. (better: наглашавати) |
| `classic_n` | класик | ok | doubtful: Класик usually names a classical author; for a classic novel the work is more naturally класично дело or класик in a looser sense. (better: класично дело) |
| `mail_v` | слати | collision | doubtful: Слати alone is just 'send'; the postal sense needs поштом, and the note lacks the perfective partner послати. (better: слати поштом) |
| `purchase_n` | куповина | collision | doubtful: The hint contains a typo, куљена instead of купљена, so a learner is shown a non-word; the term itself is fine. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: купљена ствар) |
| `purchase_v` | куповати | collision | doubtful: Куповати is correct and imperfective, but the note omits the perfective partner купити. |
| `profit_n` | добит | ok | doubtful: Добит is correct for business profit, but it is feminine with a consonant ending, so the note should say g: f. |
| `concern_v` | забрињавати | ok | doubtful: Забрињавати is correct and imperfective, but the note omits perfective partner забринути; the concern sense is fine. (better: бринути) |
| `facilities_n` | објекти | ok | doubtful: Објекти means buildings; for sports facilities at a hotel садржаји (or спортски садржаји) is more natural. (better: садржаји) |
| `grown_up_adj` | одрастао | collision | doubtful: Одрастао is acceptable, but as an adjective it means grown up in age, not mature in behaviour; hint is loose. (better: одрастао) |
| `payment_n` | плаћање | ok | doubtful: Плаћање names the act of paying, while the sense is money paid; уплата or исплата can fit, though плаћање is acceptable. (better: плаћање) |
| `equal_v` | бити једнак | ok | doubtful: Бити једнак is a copula phrase, not a verb; for arithmetic 'equals' износити or бити is more idiomatic. (better: износити) |
| `friendly_adj` | љубазан | collision | doubtful: Љубазан means polite or kind, not quite friendly; for neighbours пријатан or дружељубив is closer to the sense. (better: пријатељски настројен) |
| `smile_v` | смешити се | ok | doubtful: Смешити се is fine, but the usual perfective is осмехнути се; насмешити се is less standard. (better: осмехивати се) |
| `solo_n` | соло | collision | doubtful: Соло is right, but its -о ending suggests neuter while it is masculine; the note should give g: m. |
| `related_adj` | сродан | ok | doubtful: Сродан mostly means akin, used for things; for people being related use у сродству or рођаци. (better: у сродству) |
| `branch_v` | разгранавати се | ok | doubtful: Разгранавати се suits trees or organisations; a road that branches is more naturally рачвати се. (better: рачвати се) |
| `mess_v` | нередити | quarantined | wrong: Прљати means to make dirty only; mess up your room is about untidiness, which нередити or правити неред conveys. (better: нередити) |
| `chat_n` | разговор | collision | doubtful: Разговор is a neutral conversation; informal chat is more precisely ћаскање. (better: ћаскање) |
| `foot_n_unit_length` | фут | ok | wrong: Стопа is feminine, so note g: m is wrong; the length unit in common use is фут (masculine). (better: фут) |
| `index_n` | индекс | ok | doubtful: Book index in Serbian is usually регистар (појмова) or казало; индекс is more a university record book or economic index. (better: регистар) |
| `object_v` | противити се | collision | doubtful: Противити се means oppose, stronger than object to; приговарати fits expressing disagreement more precisely. (better: приговарати) |
| `counter_n` | пулт | ok | doubtful: Пулт suits a shop or checkout counter, but the hint 'радна површина' means work surface and misleads; better 'шалтер, пулт у продавници'. (better: пулт) |
| `interior_n` | унутрашњост | ok | doubtful: Унутрашњост is generic inside; for a decorated building or car interior ентеријер is more natural, and the g: f note is redundant with -ост. (better: ентеријер) |
| `panel_n` | панел | ok | doubtful: Панел for an expert group is an Anglicism, mostly used in panel discussion; a native term fits the sense better. (better: стручно тело) |
| `relief_n` | олакшање | ok | doubtful: Олакшање is correct, but the hint осећај лаке is truncated and ungrammatical; it should read осећај лакоће or similar. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: осећај лакоће) |
| `expansion_n` | експанзија | ok | doubtful: Експанзија is fine for business expansion, but the hint misspells 'постепаног' (should be постепеног) and 'gradual' is not implied. |
| `print_n` | штампа | collision | doubtful: Штампа mostly means the press or newspapers; for printed output or print quality, штампа is acceptable but отисак or штампани текст is clearer. (better: отисак) |
| `external_adj` | спољни | collision | doubtful: Спољни is correct, but the hint uses извана, a Croatian-flavoured adverb; Serbian says споља. |
| `aim_v` | тежити | ok | doubtful: Тежити means to strive towards (usually тежити нечему); 'aim to finish by Friday' is more naturally настојати, a plain imperfective. (better: настојати) |
| `commander_n` | командант | ok | doubtful: Командант is used for senior officers only; for a person in charge of a group or force командир is the general word. (better: командир) |
| `convention_n` | конгрес | ok | wrong: Конвенција in Serbian chiefly means a treaty or agreement; a large professional meeting is конгрес or скуп. (better: конгрес) |
| `fed_up_adj` | сит | ok | doubtful: Сит literally means full from food; 'fed up' is better as доста ми је or преко главе ми је, so the bare adjective is weak. (better: презасићен) |
| `harm_n` | штета | collision | doubtful: Штета is common but leans toward material damage; for health harm, штета по здравље works, so sense is only partly covered. (better: штета) |
| `mount_v` | расти | collision | doubtful: Расти is generic growth and the note lacks the perfective partner порасти; for tensions mounting јачати fits better. (better: јачати) |
| `extension_n` | продужење | ok | doubtful: Продужење suits a deadline extension, but the hint дужина трајања means length of duration, not the act of extending. |
| `transition_n` | прелаз | ok | doubtful: Прелаз mostly means a crossing or pass; the process of changing state is прелазак or транзиција. (better: прелазак) |
| `non_smoking_adj` | за непушаче | ok | doubtful: За непушаче is a phrase, not an adjective, and means for non-smokers; непушачки is the adjective form, as in непушачка соба. (better: непушачки) |
| `thread_n` | нит | ok | doubtful: Конац is the common word for cotton thread; нит is a strand, and the hint танка нитка is circular. (better: конац) |
| `label_n` | етикета | collision | doubtful: Етикета is right for a bottle label, but the hint трака (tape or ribbon) is misleading for a paper label. |
| `fighting_n` | борба | collision | doubtful: Борба is singular and often means struggle in the abstract; fighting as ongoing combat is more naturally борбе or сукоби. (better: борбе) |
| `racist_n` | расист | ok | doubtful: Standard Serbian usually has masculine расиста (declined like слуга); расист is also seen, so the form is less secure. (better: расиста) |
| `crack_v` | пуцати | ok | doubtful: Пуцати is imperfective and fine, but the hint 'скоро се сломити' says 'almost break', contradicting sudden breaking; напрснути/пући is more exact. (better: напрснути) |
| `mill_n` | млин | ok | doubtful: Млин is felt as Croatian-leaning; for an old mill by a river Serbian normally says воденица. (better: воденица) |
| `spin_v` | окретати се | ok | doubtful: Окретати се is a general turning around; for fast spinning in circles вртети се is more natural, as the hint itself says. (better: вртети се) |
| `vital_adj` | животан | ok | doubtful: Животан mostly means lively or relating to life; 'vital for survival' is better rendered неопходан or кључан. (better: неопходан) |
| `reverse_v` | возити уназад | ok | doubtful: Возити уназад is a phrase covering only driving backwards; it misses the change-to-opposite sense and is not a single verb. |
| `strip_v` | свлачити | ok | doubtful: Свлачити is right for undressing someone, but note omits the perfective partner свући. |
| `input_n` | допринос | collision | doubtful: Допринос suits 'your input' in discussion but not the system-input sense; hint 'унос' (data entry) is mismatched. |
| `swing_v` | љуљати се | ok | doubtful: Љуљати се means to sway or rock; the door swung open is expressed with отворити се, so the example fits poorly. |
| `ease_n` | лакоћа | ok | doubtful: Лакоћа suits the example, but the hint uses non-standard отсуство instead of одсуство and mixes comfort sense. |
| `fame_n` | слава | collision | doubtful: Слава is correct, but the hint познатост од многих is awkward, and слава also means a saint's day. |
| `blast_n` | удар | collision | doubtful: Удар is a blow or strike; for a blast of air налет, and for an explosion експлозија, fits better. (better: налет) |
| `blast_v` | пуштати | ok | doubtful: Пуштати alone just means play or let out; the loudness is missing, so it is too weak for blast. (better: пуштати гласно) |
| `chip_n` | помфрит | ok | wrong: Чипс means thin crisps, not British fish-and-chips chips; the hint even describes sticks. Помфрит is the right word. (better: помфрит) |
| `eligible_adj` | подобан | ok | doubtful: Подобан means suitable and carries a political connotation; it is not the plain term for having the right, such as испуњава услове. |
| `throat_n` | грло | ok | doubtful: Грло is right, but the hint део грла is circular and unhelpful. |
| `bloody_adj` | проклет | ok | doubtful: Проклет is acceptable as a mild intensifier, but it literally means cursed; the vague hint does not clarify the register. (better: проклет) |
| `certificate_n` | сертификат | ok | doubtful: Сертификат suits qualification certificates, but a birth certificate is извод из матичне књиге; уверење or потврда fits an official document better. (better: уверење) |
| `polish_n` | политура | ok | doubtful: Политура is mainly wood varnish; shoe polish is крема за ципеле, so the term fits the sense loosely. (better: средство за полирање) |
| `intent_adj` | усредсређен | ok | doubtful: Усредсређен means concentrated or focused, and the hint 'пун одлучности' says determined; the two do not match well. (better: напет) |
| `spare_v` | одвајати | ok | doubtful: Одвајати is imperfective separating; for sparing a few minutes the natural verb is одвојити (време) with partner note missing. (better: одвојити) |
| `utility_n` | комуналне услуге | ok | doubtful: Комуналне услуге is understandable, but Serbian has the common single word комуналије for utility bills and services. (better: комуналије) |
| `controversy_n` | контроверза | ok | doubtful: Контроверза is bookish; полемика or спор is more common for a public controversy, and 'жестока' overstates the hint. (better: полемика) |
| `trigger_n` | окидач | ok | doubtful: Окидач is the correct word for a gun trigger, but the hint calls it a 'дугме' (button) when it is a lever; hint should say 'полуга за опаљивање'. (better: оканица) |
| `venue_n` | место | collision | doubtful: Место alone is too generic and ambiguous for a concert venue; место одржавања or простор is clearer. (better: место одржавања) |
| `accuracy_n` | тачност | ok | doubtful: Тачност is correct, but the hint стање бити тачан is ungrammatical Serbian. |
| `deposit_n` | депозит | ok | doubtful: Депозит mainly means bank deposit; money paid as security, such as for rent, is usually кауција. (better: кауција) |
| `export_v` | извозити | ok | doubtful: Извозити is right, but the partner is извезти; извести (изведем) means to carry out or lead out, so the pf note is wrong. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; pf извезти not confirmed; pf извести kept (DeepSeek gives it, or wasn't asked)) |
| `jealous_adj` | љубоморан | ok | doubtful: Љубоморан mainly means jealous in love; for envy of a sister's new car завидан fits better, and the hint says as much. (better: завидан) |
| `popularity_n` | популарност | ok | doubtful: Популарност is right, but the hint стање бити вољен is ungrammatical Serbian. |
| `relate_v` | разумети | collision | doubtful: Разумети only means understand; relating to someone's frustration needs саосећати or разумети нечије осећање. (better: саосећати) |
| `graphic_adj` | експлицитан | ok | doubtful: Експлицитан is acceptable for graphic violence, but is formal and also means merely explicit; the vividness nuance is partly lost. (better: експлицитан) |
| `legendary_adj` | легендаран | ok | doubtful: Легендаран is correct, but the hint познат из легенди is too narrow for a legendary footballer. |
| `outdoor_adj` | на отвореном | ok | doubtful: На отвореном is an adverbial phrase, not an adjective; it works after a noun but is not a dictionary-form adjective as required. (better: спољашњи) |
| `palm_n` | длан | ok | doubtful: Длан is correct, but it is feminine with a consonant ending, so the note should give g: f and it is missing. |
| `video_clip_n` | видео клип | ok | doubtful: Standard orthography writes the compound as видеоклип, one word; two words видео клип is non-standard. (better: видеоклип) |
| `departure_n` | одлазак | ok | doubtful: Одлазак suits a person leaving; for a scheduled departure at noon, полазак is the natural noun. (better: полазак) |
| `explosion_n` | експлозија | ok | doubtful: Експлозија is correct, but the hint глусан is not a Serbian word, probably a typo for гласан; the hint must be fixed. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: гласан звук од пуцања) |
| `folk_n` | људи | collision | doubtful: Људи is acceptable for local folk, but it is plural-only here and the note lacks the pl marker; the sense is loose. |
| `generate_v` | генерисати | ok | doubtful: Генерисати is a calque from English; for generating income стварати or остваривати is more natural, and the note lacks aspect information. (better: стварати) |
| `magical_adj` | магичан | ok | doubtful: Магичан means supernatural or of magic; the example 'magical evening' is figurative and чаробан is the natural everyday word. (better: чаробан) |
| `outfit_n` | одело | collision | doubtful: Одело normally means a formal suit; an outfit of mixed clothes is better rendered комплет or гардероба. (better: комплет одеће) |
| `slight_n` | увреда | ok | doubtful: Увреда is a stronger insult; a slight is a snub or disrespect, closer to омаловажавање or пренебрегавање. (better: омаловажавање) |
| `twist_v` | окретати | ok | doubtful: Окретати is generic turning; for twisting a cap off, одвртати (pf: одвинути) is more precise and natural. (better: одвртати) |
| `clinic_n` | клиника | ok | doubtful: In Serbia клиника usually means a university hospital department; a clinic for a checkup is амбуланта or дом здравља. (better: амбуланта) |
| `disability_n` | инвалидност | ok | doubtful: Инвалидитет is the standard abstract noun for disability; инвалидност is rarer and sounds awkward. (better: инвалидитет) |
| `highlight_v` | наглашавати | collision | doubtful: Наглашавати means to stress or emphasise; for highlighting key points истицати (pf истаћи) is the more natural verb. (better: истицати) |
| `holder_n` | носилац | ok | wrong: Држач is a physical holder for objects; a title holder is носилац титуле, so this is the wrong sense. (better: носилац) |
| `poet_n` | песник | ok | doubtful: Песник is right, but the plural песници is regular, so the pl note is wrongly used for an irregular plural. |
| `attract_v` | привлачити | ok | doubtful: Term is the right imperfective verb, but the note omits the perfective partner (привући) that should be listed. (better: привлачити) |
| `implement_v` | спроводити | collision | doubtful: Спроводити is a good imperfective for implementing rules, but the note omits the perfective partner спровести. (better: спроводити) |
| `blade_n` | сечиво | ok | doubtful: Сечиво is correct but more technical; оштрица is the common word for the sharp blade of a knife. (better: оштрица) |
| `humour_n` | хумор | ok | doubtful: Хумор is right, but the hint 'способност да се буде смех' is ungrammatical nonsense; should be смисао за шалу. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: смисао за шалу) |
| `film_maker_n` | филмски стваралац | ok | doubtful: Филмски стваралац is a clumsy two-word formal phrase; режисер or филмаџија is the natural everyday term. (better: режисер) |
| `identification_n` | лична карта | collision | doubtful: Лична карта is specifically an ID card; identification as any proof of identity is more broadly лична исправа. (better: лична исправа) |
| `restore_v` | обнављати | ok | doubtful: Обнављати means to renew or renovate; restoring an old painting is рестаурирати, though обнављати is acceptable in a general sense. (better: рестаурирати) |
| `artistic_adj` | уметнички | ok | doubtful: Уметнички means of art; for a person with artistic talent the phrase надарен за уметност fits better. (better: уметнички настројен) |
| `attendance_n` | посета | collision | doubtful: Посета means a visit; the number of people present at an event is посећеност, so the term fits the sense poorly. (better: посећеност) |
| `beloved_adj` | вољен | ok | doubtful: Вољен is correct but for a singer loved by fans омиљен is the more natural adjective. (better: омиљен) |
| `bless_v` | благословљати | ok | doubtful: Благословљати is rare; the usual imperfective is благосиљати (pf благословити), and the hint 'чинити светим' is clumsy. (better: благосиљати) |
| `decrease_v` | смањивати се | ok | doubtful: Term is right and imperfective, but the note should give the perfective partner: pf: смањити се. (better: смањивати се) |
| `testimony_n` | сведочанство | ok | doubtful: Сведочанство leans toward testament or proof; a witness's formal statement in court is исказ or сведочење. (better: исказ) |
| `gentleman_n` | господин | collision | doubtful: Господин means Mr or sir in general; a polite, well-mannered man is a џентлмен. (better: џентлмен) |
| `presidency_n` | председништво | ok | doubtful: Председништво usually means the presidium or collective presidency body; for holding the office, функција председника or председништво in context is less clear. (better: функција председника) |
| `rocky_adj` | каменист | ok | doubtful: Каменит is the more usual Serbian adjective for stony terrain; каменист is rarer and feels non-standard. (better: каменит) |
| `sometime_adv` | некада | ok | doubtful: Некада mainly means once or formerly; for an unspecified future time, as in the example, некад or једног дана is more natural. (better: некад) |
| `bacon_n` | сланина | ok | doubtful: Сланина is the common word for bacon in Serbia, but the hint усољена свињска маст describes lard, not bacon. |
| `cattle_n` | говедо | ok | doubtful: English cattle is a plural collective; singular говедо means a single animal, so the plural говеда or collective стока is more accurate. (better: говеда) |
| `eliminate_v` | елиминисати | ok | doubtful: Елиминисати is biaspectual and the hint gives a perfective; уклањати with pf уклонити is a clean imperfective fit. (better: уклањати) |
| `tackle_v` | решавати | collision | doubtful: Решавати means to solve; tackle stresses starting to confront a problem, which the hint admits, so the term and gloss mismatch. (better: ухватити се укоштац) |
| `addiction_n` | зависност | ok | doubtful: Зависност is the right noun, but it is feminine with a consonant ending, so the note should give g: f. |
| `horn_n` | труба | quarantined | wrong: Сирена is a siren, not a car horn; the hint also uses Russian-style 'устройства' instead of Serbian 'уређаја'. (better: труба) |
| `invitation_n` | позивница | ok | doubtful: Позивница is specifically a printed card; general request to attend is позив, so the hint also overspecifies. (better: позив) |
| `lean_adj` | мршав | ok | doubtful: Мршав means skinny, often negative; 'lean and fit' is better витак or мишићав, and the hint 'без масти' is vague. (better: витак) |
| `lean_v` | наслањати се | ok | doubtful: Наслањати се is right, but the hint ослањати се means rely on, a different sense, and could mislead. |
| `meaningful_adj` | смислен | ok | doubtful: Смислен means sensible or coherent; for a meaningful conversation садржајан or значајан better conveys depth and importance. (better: садржајан) |
| `alike_adv` | слично | ok | doubtful: Слично means 'similarly'; 'behaved alike' is better as подједнако or исто, and the hint 'на исти начин' differs from the term. (better: подједнако) |
| `keen_adj` | заинтересован | collision | doubtful: Заинтересован means interested only; keen on learning is better conveyed by жељан or ентузијастичан, and the hint is redundant. (better: жељан) |
| `courtesy_n` | учтивост | ok | doubtful: Term is fine, but note 'g: f' is unnecessary since the -ост ending already shows feminine gender. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept) |
| `prospect_n` | изглед | collision | doubtful: Изглед mostly means view or appearance; the 'chance' sense is normally plural изгледи, so шанса or могућност is clearer. (better: шанса) |
| `retreat_n` | уточиште | collision | doubtful: Уточиште means refuge or sanctuary; a mountain retreat for a stay is closer to склониште or одмаралиште. (better: склониште) |
| `separation_n` | раздвајање | ok | doubtful: Раздвајање is fine, but the hint стање бити одвојен is ungrammatical; раздвојеност names the state better. |
| `athletic_adj` | атлетски | ok | doubtful: Атлетски means relating to athletics; for a person who is physically strong and active, атлетичан is more natural. (better: атлетичан) |
| `appreciation_n` | цењење | quarantined | wrong: Поштовање means respect, not understanding and enjoyment of quality; цењење or уважавање fits appreciation of art. (better: цењење) |
| `dive_n` | скок | collision | doubtful: Bare скок means any jump; the sense is a head-first dive into water, so скок у воду is more precise. (better: скок у воду) |
| `heal_v` | зарастати | ok | doubtful: Зарастати fits wounds only, not general recovery from illness, and the note lacks perfective partner зарасти. (better: зарастати) |
| `alternate_v` | смењивати се | ok | doubtful: Смењивати се is the right verb, but the hint merely repeats the term and gives a learner no extra information. |
| `grip_n` | хват | ok | doubtful: Хват is a technical term from sport and wrestling; стисак is the everyday word for a firm grip on a rope. (better: стисак) |
| `preference_n` | преференција | ok | doubtful: Преференција is mostly economic or formal; for liking tea over coffee склоност or наклоност is more natural. (better: склоност) |
| `proportion_n` | удео | ok | doubtful: Пропорција means ratio or proportionality; 'a share of a whole' is better rendered by удео. (better: удео) |
| `faithful_adj` | веран | collision | doubtful: Веран is right for loyal, but the hint одани has a definite form; the indefinite одан is expected. |
| `booth_n` | тезга | collision | doubtful: Тезга is a counter or stall table, not an enclosed space as the hint says; штанд or киоск fits a market booth better. (better: штанд) |
| `import_v` | увозити | ok | doubtful: Term is right, but the perfective of увозити in the sense of importing is увезти, not увести (to lead in); the note is wrong. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; pf увезти not confirmed; pf увести kept (DeepSeek gives it, or wasn't asked)) |
| `lung_n` | плућа | ok | doubtful: Плућа is a plural-only noun, so the note must be g: pl; it is missing, which would mislead learners. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; g: pl added) |
| `rental_n` | најам | ok | doubtful: Најам is the act of hiring; the sense is a payment, which is закупнина or кирија. (better: закупнина) |
| `darling_n` | драги | ok | doubtful: Драги is masculine only (feminine драга) and as an address душо or драга is more natural; the note g: m is limiting. (better: драги) |
| `servant_n` | слуга | ok | doubtful: Слуга ends in -а yet is masculine; the note needs 'g: m', otherwise learners will treat it as feminine. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept) |
| `traffic_jam_n` | застој | ok | doubtful: Застој alone means a stoppage in general; the usual phrase for a traffic jam is гужва у саобраћају. (better: гужва у саобраћају) |
| `bud_n` | пупољак | ok | doubtful: Пупољак is the correct word, but the hint млади изданак means young shoot, which is a different thing. |
| `lodge_n` | колиба | collision | doubtful: Колиба suggests a simple hut or cabin; a lodge is usually a larger guest cabin, so match is only approximate. (better: колиба) |
| `rebel_v` | бунити се | ok | doubtful: Бунити се is right and imperfective, but the note omits the perfective partner побунити се. |
| `unbelievable_adj` | невероватан | collision | doubtful: Невероватан is correct, but the hint merely repeats the term and helps no learner; use например тешко веродостојан. |
| `bio_n` | биографија | ok | doubtful: Term is right, but the entry duplicates the longer biography sense; hint 'кратка биографија' is just a restatement. (better: биографија) |
| `excellence_n` | одличност | quarantined | wrong: Hint uses квалитета, a Croatian feminine form; Serbian quality is masculine квалитет. Изврсност also leans Croatian. (better: одличност) |
| `graduation_n` | дипломирање | ok | doubtful: Дипломирање is the act of graduating, not the ceremony; the ceremony is called промоција or додела диплома. (better: промоција) |
| `miserable_adj` | несрећан | collision | doubtful: Term is fine, but the hint 'вело несретан' uses Ijekavian несретан and non-Serbian вело; should be веома несрећан. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: јадан) |
| `scan_n` | скен | ok | doubtful: Скен is a colloquial calque; скенирана копија or скениран документ is more standard in writing. (better: скенирана копија) |
| `span_v` | трајати | collision | doubtful: Трајати covers the duration sense but loses the idea of covering a range; also lacks a perfective partner note. (better: трајати) |
| `presumably_adv` | вероватно | collision | doubtful: Вероватно means probably and is acceptable, but the hint 'као да је вероватно' is awkward and conveys seeming rather than presuming. (better: по свој прилици) |
| `adequate_adj` | довољан | collision | doubtful: Довољан means enough; adequate in the sense of suitable is better адекватан or одговарајући. (better: адекватан) |
| `dip_v` | умакати | ok | doubtful: Perfective partner is умочити; умакнути means to slip away, so the note would teach a wrong verb. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; pf умочити not confirmed; pf умакнути kept (DeepSeek gives it, or wasn't asked)) |
| `interact_v` | интераговати | ok | doubtful: Term is correct but the note omits the perfective partner; also the hint is a wordy gloss rather than a short one. (better: интераговати) |
| `mobility_n` | покретљивост | ok | doubtful: Term is fine, but the note 'g: f' is redundant because the -ост ending already shows feminine gender. (better: покретљивост) |
| `stack_n` | гомила | collision | doubtful: Гомила is a heap; a neat stack of books is стос, and the hint нагомилана ствари is ungrammatical. (better: стос) |
| `supportive_adj` | подржавајући | ok | doubtful: Подржавајући is a calque and stiff; Serbian prefers a phrase such as који пружа подршку or пун разумевања. (better: који пружа подршку) |
| `thesis_n` | дисертација | quarantined | wrong: Теза means a proposition or claim, not a degree paper; use дисертација or завршни рад. (better: дисертација) |
| `tide_n` | плима | ok | doubtful: Плима means only high tide; the regular rise and fall is плима и осека, and морска доба is ungrammatical. (better: плима и осека) |
| `withdrawal_n` | подизање | ok | doubtful: Подизање is correct for cash withdrawal, but the hint вађење новца is unnatural; подизање новца would be better. |
| `intermediate_adj` | средњи | collision | doubtful: Средњи alone is ambiguous (middle, secondary school); the hint helps, but 'средњег нивоа' is the idiomatic usage. (better: средњи) |
| `likewise_adv` | такође | collision | doubtful: Такође means also; the sense and example (I feel likewise) call for the same way, so исто тако fits better. (better: исто тако) |
| `nut_n` | орах | ok | doubtful: Орах specifically means walnut; the general hard-shelled edible seed is орашасти плод, with ораси as plural. (better: орашасти плод) |
| `strengthen_v` | јачати | ok | doubtful: Јачати is correct and imperfective, but the note omits the perfective partner ојачати that learners need. (better: јачати) |
| `transform_v` | трансформисати | ok | doubtful: Трансформисати is biaspectual and the note gives no partner; преображавати (pf преобразити) is a clearer imperfective with partner. (better: преображавати) |
| `eager_adj` | жељан | ok | doubtful: Жељан takes a genitive noun and means desiring; eager to start a job is better нестрпљив or једва чекао. (better: нестрпљив) |
| `heart_warming_adj` | дирљив | collision | doubtful: Дирљив means touching and is acceptable, but misses the warm, uplifting nuance; топао or дирљив both approximate. (better: дирљив) |
| `ladder_n` | мердевине | ok | doubtful: Term мердевине is the right common word, but it is plural-only, so the note must be "g: pl", not "g: f". (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; g: pl added) |
| `accordingly_adv` | према томе | ok | doubtful: Према томе means therefore; the sense here is appropriately, which у складу с тим or сходно томе expresses. (better: у складу с тим) |
| `complexity_n` | сложеност | ok | doubtful: Сложеност is right, but the hint особина бити компликован is ungrammatical; use особина онога што је сложено. |
| `slam_v` | лупати | collision | doubtful: Лупати means to bang repeatedly; slamming a door once is залупити (pf), and the note lacks a partner. (better: залупити) |
| `nerves_n` | нерви | ok | doubtful: Нерви is right for anxiety, but the note should mark it plural-only with 'g: pl'; the empty note is incomplete. (better: нерви) |
| `rider_n` | јахач | ok | doubtful: Јахач means only a horse rider; the sense includes bike riders, who are бициклисти. |
| `shipping_n` | превоз | collision | doubtful: Превоз means transport generally; shipping costs in the example suggest отпрема or достава, and the hint repeats превоз. (better: отпрема) |
| `junk_food_n` | нездрава храна | ok | wrong: Брза храна means fast food, which is a different concept; junk food is нездрава храна. (better: нездрава храна) |
| `pad_n` | јастучић | ok | doubtful: Јастучић is masculine (ending -ић), so the note 'g: n' is wrong; the term itself is acceptable for a small pad. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept) |
| `disposal_n` | одлагање | ok | doubtful: Одлагање mainly means postponing or depositing; the hint 'чишћење отпада' is also an odd gloss for getting rid of. (better: уклањање) |
| `upside_down_adj` | наопако | ok | doubtful: Наопако is an adverb, not an adjective, and the standard form is наопако/наопачке; the learner gets no adjective use. (better: наопачке) |
| `clause_n` | клаузула | quarantined | doubtful: Клаузула is mainly a contract or legal clause; the grammatical part of a sentence is not well served by this word. (better: клауза) |
| `expectation_n` | очекивање | ok | doubtful: Term is right, but the hint is ungrammatical (should be 'да ће се нешто догодити') and 'увереност' overstates the belief. (better: очекивање) |
| `resign_v` | подносити оставку | ok | doubtful: Fixed expression is natural, but note lacks the perfective partner (поднети оставку) and the hint 'напустити положај' is loose. (better: подносити оставку) |
| `superintendent_n` | начелник | ok | doubtful: Начелник means head of a department; it does not clearly convey a police or school superintendent, so the sense is vague. (better: надзорник) |
| `slap_v` | шамарати | ok | doubtful: Шамарати is correct and imperfective, but note omits perfective partner ошамарити. |
| `therapist_n` | терапеут | ok | doubtful: Терапеут is a general therapist; the sense given, mental illness, is more precisely психотерапеут. (better: психотерапеут) |
| `nominee_n` | кандидат | collision | doubtful: Кандидат means candidate generally; for a nominee for a prize номиновани is closer to the sense. (better: номинованик) |
| `pirate_n` | пират | ok | doubtful: Гусар is the traditional, most common Serbian word for a sea pirate; пират is used but less natural. (better: гусар) |
| `wit_n` | духовитост | ok | doubtful: Духовитост is right, but the note g: f is redundant since the -ост ending shows gender, against the convention. |
| `absurd_adj` | апсурдан | ok | doubtful: Term is right, but the hint 'веома глуп' means very stupid, which misstates absurd as nonsensical or illogical. (better: апсурдан) |
| `philosophical_adj` | стоички | ok | wrong: Филозофски means relating to philosophy, not calm acceptance of bad news; стоички or смирен conveys this sense. (better: стоички) |
| `scheduled_adj` | предвиђен | ok | doubtful: Предвиђен means foreseen or expected; for scheduled to arrive предвиђен works but заказан or редован fits timetable sense better. (better: заказан) |
| `gum_n` | десни | collision | doubtful: Десни is the plural of десна and used as a plural-only noun, so the note should be g: pl; it is missing. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; g: pl added) |
| `fierce_adj` | жесток | ok | doubtful: Жесток means cruel or brutal, which suits people; for a violent storm силовит or јак is more natural. (better: силовит) |
| `hail_v` | дозивати | ok | doubtful: Дозивати means repeated calling out; hailing a taxi is better заустављати (такси), and the hint is perfective. (better: заустављати) |
| `glow_n` | сјај | ok | doubtful: Сјај means shine or brightness; for the warm glow of embers, жар is more exact, and the hint is about steady heat. (better: жар) |
| `necessity_n` | потреба | collision | doubtful: Потреба is the same term as for 'need' with an identical hint; for necessity, неопходност or нужда distinguishes it better. (better: неопходност) |
| `offshore_adj` | приобални | quarantined | wrong: Hint 'насељен на мору' means inhabited at sea, which is wrong; офшорни is also a loan mostly for tax havens, приобални/на мору fits. (better: приобални) |
| `landing_n` | слетање | ok | doubtful: Слетање is the correct term, but the hint 'додир авиона' (touch of the plane) is vague; 'спуштање авиона на тло' is clearer. |
| `obsession_n` | опседнутост | ok | doubtful: Опседнутост describes the state of being possessed; опсесија is the usual noun for an obsession, and the g: f note is redundant. (better: опсесија) |
| `unlock_v` | откључавати | ok | doubtful: Term and aspect pair are right, but the hint 'скидати браву' means removing the lock itself, which is wrong. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: отварати браву) |
| `browser_n` | прегледач | ok | doubtful: Прегледач is understood, but претраживач is the more common everyday word in Serbia for a web browser. (better: претраживач) |
| `imprisonment_n` | затвор | collision | doubtful: Затвор alone is mainly the prison building; in the sense of facing imprisonment казна затвора is more precise. (better: казна затвора) |
| `ranger_n` | чувар природе | ok | doubtful: Descriptive phrase works but there is no single established term; шумар or чувар парка also used. (better: чувар природе) |
| `economist_n` | економиста | ok | doubtful: Економиста is valid, but it is masculine with an -а ending, so the note should give g: m and it is missing. |
| `prompt_n` | подсетник | collision | doubtful: Подсетник means a memo or reminder note; for a cue given to an actor it is imprecise. (better: наговештај) |
| `prompt_v` | подстицати | collision | doubtful: Подстицати fits 'encourage', but the note lacks the perfective partner подстаћи, which verbs need. |
| `successive_adj` | узастопни | ok | doubtful: Узастопни is the definite form; the dictionary indefinite masculine lemma is узастопан, per the adjective convention. (better: узастопан) |
| `undergraduate_n` | студент | collision | doubtful: Студент alone does not distinguish undergraduates from postgraduates; the hint is also inaccurate, since many have not yet graduated anywhere. (better: студент основних студија) |
| `outrage_n` | негодовање | ok | doubtful: Негодовање is expressed disapproval; огорчење better conveys public outrage as strong shocked anger. (better: огорчење) |
| `paperwork_n` | папирологија | ok | doubtful: Папирологија is colloquial and jocular; neutral registers prefer администрација or папири for administrative paperwork. (better: администрација) |
| `gateway_n` | улаз | collision | doubtful: Улаз is a plain entrance; for a figurative or architectural gateway to the old city капија is more precise. (better: капија) |
| `decay_v` | трулити | ok | doubtful: Трулити fits rotting wood, but the note omits the perfective partner иструлити and the sense is narrower than decay. |
| `imagery_n` | сликовитост | ok | doubtful: Сликовитост is acceptable for literary imagery, but the g: f note is redundant because -ост already shows feminine gender. |
| `imply_v` | наговештавати | ok | doubtful: Наговештавати suits implying indirectly, but the note lacks the perfective partner наговестити. (better: наговештавати) |
| `naughty_adj` | неваљао | ok | doubtful: Неваљао is right, but the hint размажен means spoiled, which is a different meaning from mischievous. |
| `obey_v` | поштовати | collision | doubtful: Поштовати means respect; obeying orders is слушати or покоравати се, and the hint uses a perfective verb; no pf given. (better: покоравати се) |
| `ramp_n` | рампа | ok | doubtful: Рампа mostly means barrier or loading dock; for a wheelchair ramp, нагнута раван or рампа за колица is clearer. (better: нагнута раван) |
| `scent_n` | мирис | quarantined | doubtful: Мирис is right, but hint задах denotes a bad smell or breath, contradicting pleasant; use пријатан мирис. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint dropped: no reliable hint) |
